## LU-KYÜÔ DJÜN FOH-ING SHÜ. <u>路加</u>傳福音書

### Lk. I.

<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>KYI-JÜN</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>好幾個</rb><rt>hao-kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>動手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>*cong*</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>篤實</rb><rt>toh-jih</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>件打件</rb><rt>gyin-tang-gyin</rt><rb>寫落</rb><rt>sia-lôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>傳道個</rb><rt>djün-dao-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>親眼</rb><rt>tsʽing-ngæn</rt><rb>看見過</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt><rb>傳落來</rb><rt>djün-lôh-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從頭硬節</rb><rt>dzong-deo-ngeng-tsih</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>仔細</rb><rt>ts-si</rt><rb>查過</rb><rt>dzô-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>挨次序</rb><rt>a-tsʽ-jü</rt><rb>寫出來</rb><rt>sia-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>提阿非羅</rb><rt>Di-o-fi-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大人</rb><rt>da-jing</rt></ruby>，<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>學過個</rb><rt>ʽôh-ko-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>實惠個</rb><rt>jih-we-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>HYI-LIH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<u><ruby><rb>亞比亞</rb><rt>Üô-pi-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒迦利亞</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞倫</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利沙伯</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô-pah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>看來</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>禮節</rb><rt>li-tsih</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>批評</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利沙伯</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再話</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>老了</rb><rt>lao-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Pi* *da-ka* *eo* “*Yiæ-su* *kyüông-seng* *keh* *nyin*,” *tsao* *loh* *nyin*. 比大概謳「<u>耶穌</u>降生箇年」早六年。（原註）</sup>

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒迦利亞</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>挨班個</rb><rt>a-pæn-go</rt><rb>次序</rb><rt>tsʽ-jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>祭司個</rb><rt>tsi-s-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>祭司個</rb><rt>tsi-s-go</rt><rb>規矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抽籤</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽin</rt><rb>抽着</rb><rt>tsʽiu-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>點</rb><rt>tin</rt><rb>香</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>點</rb><rt>Tin</rt><rb>香</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>現出來</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>香壇個</rb><rt>hyiang-dæn-go</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒迦利亞</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>懍懍動</rb><rt>ling-ling-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>怕起來了</rb><rt>pʽô-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>撒迦利亞</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>聽聞了</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利沙伯</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>爾自</rb><rt>Ng-zi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>快活</rb><rt>kwʽa-weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>生出來</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Dziu</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>看來</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老酒</rb><rt>lao-tsiu</rt><rb>燒酒</rb><rt>siao-tsiu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>胎</rb><rt>tʽæ</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>受了</rb><rt>ziu-leh</rt><rb>滿足了</rb><rt>mun-tsoh-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>歸服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt><rb>性情</rb><rt>sing-dzing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依順個</rb><rt>i-jing-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>獲</rb><rt>ʽôh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>一班</rb><rt>ih-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>用個</rb><rt>yüong-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒迦利亞</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我自</rb><rt>Ngô-zi</rt><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>大了</rb><rt>do-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>老了</rb><rt>lao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>*yiu-go*</rt></ruby>？」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加百列</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>信息</rb><rt>sing-sih</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>Z-ʽeo</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效驗</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>卻勒</rb><rt>kyʽiah-leh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啞子</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做成個</rb><rt>tso-dzing-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>侍候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><u><ruby><rb>撒迦利亞</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>耽擱間</rb><rt>tæn-kôh-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt><rb>走出來了</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>顯靈</rb><rt>hyin-ling</rt><rb>看見過</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>裝</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>手勢</rb><rt>siu-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>竟是介</rb><rt>kying-z-ka</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>啞子</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 侍候：等候</sup>

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>臨滿</rb><rt>ling-mun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利沙伯</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>身孕</rb><rt>sing-yüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>尋尋</rb><rt>zing-zing</rt><rb>躲避</rb><rt>to-bi</rt><rb>五個</rb><rt>ng-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>25</sup> 「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>照顧</rb><rt>tsiao-kwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*ngô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>TAO</rt><rb>第六個</rb><rt>di-loh-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加百列</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>省</rb><rt>*sang*</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>許</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇家個</rb><rt>keh-kô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>Teh-djôh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恭喜</rb><rt>kong-hyi</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>朵化</rb><rt>tô-hwô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>𢖺𢖺動</rb><rt>yiah-yiah-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>？

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>受胎</rb><rt>ziu-tʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇族</rb><rt>keh-dzoh</rt><rb>世世代代個</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>末</rb><rt>mah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>降落來</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>遮蔭</rb><rt>tsô-ing</rt><rb>爾儂</rb><rt>ng-noh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>聖善個</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>*Na-hwun*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>親眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利沙伯</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô-pah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>老了</rb><rt>lao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>身孕</rb><rt>sing-yüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>六個</rb><rt>loh-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>別開</rb><rt>bih-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>省</rb><rt>*sang*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒迦利亞</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利沙伯</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô-pah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以利沙伯</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>*sing-hyiang*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>胎</rb><rt>tʽæ</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跳起來</rb><rt>tʽiao-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以利沙伯</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>受了</rb><rt>ziu-leh</rt><rb>滿足了</rb><rt>mun-tsoh-de</rt></ruby>，<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>胎裏</rb><rt>tʽæ-li</rt><rb>生出來個</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>*hao-cʽü*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>？<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>耳朵裏</rb><rt>ng-to-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>胎</rb><rt>tʽæ</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歡歡喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt><rb>跳起來</rb><rt>tʽiao-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>*Cü-go*</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效驗</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>。<sup> </sup>」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, Ng siang-sing Cü-go shih-wô yiu yiao-nyiæn, tao yiu foh-kyʽi. 或話，爾相信主個說話有效驗，倒有福氣。（原註）</sup>

<sup>46</sup> <u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，

「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>尊重</rb><rt>tseng-djong</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>靈魂</rb><rt>ling-weng</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>救主</rb><rt>Kyiu-cü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>歡歡喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看顧</rb><rt>kʽen-kwu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>低微個</rb><rt>ti-vi-go</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>從今以後</rb><rt>dzong-kying-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>世世代代</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聖善個</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>Pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>哀憐</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世世代代</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>51</sup> <ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>手梗</rb><rt>siu-kwang</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>勁道</rb><rt>gying-dao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>驕傲</rb><rt>kyiao-ngao</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>七丁八倒</rb><rt>tsʽih-ting-pah-tao</rt></ruby>。<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>推</rb><rt>tʽe</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>低微</rb><rt>ti-vi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>舉</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>肚飢</rb><rt>Du-kyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>給</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>喫飽</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>富足個</rb><rt>fu-tsoh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>空手</rb><rt>kʽong-siu</rt><rb>歸去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>54</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>記念</rb><rt>kyi-nyiæn</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世世代代</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>當初</rb><rt>*tông-tsʽu*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>道個</rb><rt>dao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>扶助</rb><rt>vu-dzu</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>56</sup> <u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利沙伯</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大約</rb><rt>da-iah</rt><rb>庉了</rb><rt>deng-leh</rt><rb>三個</rb><rt>sæn-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>57</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以利沙伯</rb><rt>YI-LI-SÔ-PAH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生姆</rb><rt>sang-m</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>58</sup> <ruby><rb>鄰舍家</rb><rt>Ling-sô-kô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>親眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>歡喜相</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-siang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>59</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>第八</rb><rt>di-pah</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打賬</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒迦利亞</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>60</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>61</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>親眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>。」<sup>62</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>裝裝</rb><rt>tsông-tsông</rt><rb>手勢</rb><rt>siu-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>63</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>*ah-tia*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>粉板</rb><rt>feng-pæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>寫出來</rb><rt>sia-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>。」 <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>64</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒迦利亞</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>舌頭</rb><rt>zih-deo</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>開開</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>講起來</rb><rt>kông-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝讚</rb><rt>coh-tsæn</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>65</sup> <ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>鄰舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>怕起來</rb><rt>pʽô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>各到處</rb><rt>kôh-tao-cʽü</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>話開去了</rb><rt>wô-kʽæ-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>65</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>*keh-sing*</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt><rb>記勒</rb><rt>kyi-leh</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>！」<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>親手</rb><rt>tsʽing-siu</rt><rb>朵化</rb><rt>tô-hwô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>67</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒迦利亞</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>受了</rb><rt>ziu-leh</rt><rb>滿足了</rb><rt>mun-tsoh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，

<sup>68</sup> 「<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>Kæ</rt><rb>祝讚</rb><rt>coh-tsæn</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看顧</rb><rt>kʽen-kwu</rt><rb>取贖</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<sup>69</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇家</rb><rt>keh-kô</rt><rb>舉上</rb><rt>kyü-zông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>救世個</rb><rt>kyiu-shü-go</rt><rb>角</rb><rt>kôh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<sup>70</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>脫出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>惡愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>72</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*Keh*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>記念</rb><rt>kyi-nyiæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>聖善個</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>，<sup>73</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>罰過個</rb><rt>vah-ko-go</rt><rb>咒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，<sup>74</sup> <ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>脫出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>仇敵個</rb><rt>dziu-dih-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<sup>75</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>清潔</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyih</rt><rb>一生一世</rb><rt>ih-sang-ih-si</rt><rb>放膽</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>76</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<sup>77</sup> <ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>救個</rb><rt>kyiu-go</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>fông-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>慈悲心</rb><rt>dz-pe-sing</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>78</sup> <ruby><rb>虧勒</rb><rt>Kyʽü-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>慈悲</rb><rt>dz-pe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>出去個</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>日光</rb><rt>Nyih-kwông</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djôh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<sup>79</sup> <ruby><rb>照着</rb><rt>tsiao-djôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>陰司</rb><rt>ing-s</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>遮暗</rb><rt>tsô-en</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>筆直</rb><rt>pih-dzih</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>太平個</rb><rt>tʽa-bing-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>80</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>大起來</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靈性</rb><rt>ling-sing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>*pah-sing*</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。

### Lk. II.

<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>KEH-GO</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>該撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>亞古士督</rb><rt>Üô-kwu-z-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>上諭</rb><rt>zông-yü</rt><rb>頒落來</rb><rt>pæn-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>天下人</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô-nying</rt><rb>通</rb><rt>tʽong</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>挨戶冊</rb><rt>a-wu-tsʽah</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> （<u><ruby><rb>居里扭</rb><rt>Kyü-li-nyiu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>敘利亞</rb><rt>Jü-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>總督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>挨戶冊</rb><rt>a-wu-tsʽah</rt><rb>剛剛</rb><rt>kông-kông</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>。）<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>Kôh-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>本城</rb><rt>peng-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>冊子</rb><rt>tsʽah-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4, 5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族裏</rb><rt>dzoh-li</rt><rb>箇派</rb><rt>keh-pʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>定間個</rb><rt>ding-kæn-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>大肚個</rb><rt>do-du-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>省</rb><rt>*sang*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯利恆</rb><rt>Pah-li-ʽeng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>冊子</rb><rt>tsʽah-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>庉間</rb><rt>deng-kæn</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生姆</rb><rt>sang-m</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>頭一個</rb><rt>deo-ih-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>布</rb><rt>pu</rt><rb>裹好仔</rb><rt>ko-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>睏勒</rb><rt>kwʽeng-leh</rt><rb>馬槽裏</rb><rt>mô-zao-li</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>下處裏</rb><rt>ʽô-cʽü-li</rt><rb>嘸采</rb><rt>m-tsʽæ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>庉了</rb><rt>deng-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>Keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>看羊個</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>夜頭</rb><rt>yia-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天外</rb><rt>tʽin-nga</rt><rb>調班</rb><rt>diao-pæn</rt><rb>巡更</rb><rt>jing-kang</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>忽而然</rb><rt>Hweh-r-jün</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>團圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>照着</rb><rt>tsiao-djôh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弄大</rb><rt>long-do</rt><rb>嚇倒了</rb><rt>hah-tao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>Hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>歡喜個</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-go</rt><rb>信息</rb><rt>sing-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>信息</rb><rt>sing-sih</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>關着</rb><rt>kwæn-djôh</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>今末子</rb><rt>Kyih-mih-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>救主</rb><rt>Kyiu-cü</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>生出來</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>尋着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>布</rb><rt>pu</rt><rb>裹的</rb><rt>ko-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馬槽裏</rb><rt>mô-zao-li</rt><rb>睏間</rb><rt>kwʽeng-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>記認</rb><rt>kyi-nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>忽而然</rb><rt>Hweh-r-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>天裏個</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>兵將</rb><rt>ping-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>Wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>14</sup> 「<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>*tʽin*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地下</rb><rt>di-ʽô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>太平</rb><rt>tʽa-bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, læ-tih kwu-djôh. 或話，來的顧着。（原註）</sup>

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>看羊個</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯利恆</rb><rt>Pah-li-ʽeng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>果然</rb><rt>*ko-jün*</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>馬槽裏</rb><rt>mô-zao-li</rt><rb>睏間個</rb><rt>kwʽeng-kæn-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>看過仔</rb><rt>Kʽen-ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>*tʽin-s*</rt><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>詳細</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt><rb>話出來了</rb><rt>wô-cʽih-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>看羊個</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>存的</rb><rt>dzeng-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不常</rb><rt>peh-djông</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>看羊個</rb><rt>Kʽen-yiang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>歸去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>*tʽin-s*</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>依路</rb><rt>*i-lu*</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>第八</rb><rt>di-pah</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>受胎</rb><rt>ziu-tʽæ</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Pi* *da-kæ* *eo* “*Yiæ-su* *kyüông-seng* *keh* *nyin*” *tsao* *s* *nyin*. 比大概謳「<u>耶穌</u>降生箇年」早四年。（原註）</sup>

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>TSIAO</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>潔淨個</rb><rt>kyih-zing-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>滿了</rb><rt>mun-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抱勒</rb><rt>bao-leh</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>獻上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<sup>23</sup> （<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>寫落</rb><rt>sia-lôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>律法裏</rb><rt>lih-fah-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>頭一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>生出個</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-go</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>）<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽOh-tsia</rt><rb>一雙</rb><rt>ih-shông</rt><rb>斑鳩</rb><rt>pæn-kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>兩隻</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>鵓鴿</rb><rt>beh-keh</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西面</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>虔心</rb><rt>gyin-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>安慰</rb><rt>en-w̆e</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>暗暗</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西面</rb><rt>*Si-min*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>聖靈個</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling-go</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爹孃</rb><rt>tia-nyiang</rt><rb>抱勒</rb><rt>bao-leh</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>走進來</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>律法個</rb><rt>lih-fah-go</rt><rb>規矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西面</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>抱仔</rb><rt>bao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝讚</rb><rt>coh-tsæn</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，

<sup>29</sup> 「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>釋放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>安安穩穩</rb><rt>en-en-w̆eng-w̆eng</rt><rb>去世</rb><rt>kyʽü-shü</rt></ruby>，<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>親眼</rb><rt>tsʽing-ngæn</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>救星</rb><rt>kyiu-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>萬國</rb><rt>væn-koh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>設立個</rb><rt>shih-lih-go</rt></ruby>，<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照着</rb><rt>tsiao-djôh</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>點着</rb><rt>tin-djôh</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西面</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>*Ng-ts*</rt><rb>立落來</rb><rt>lih-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>兆頭</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>扳駁</rb><rt>pæn-poh</rt></ruby>，<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>露出來</rb><rt>lu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>爾自個</rb><rt>ng-zi-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>*ziang*</rt><rb>寶劍</rb><rt>pao-kyin</rt><rb>穿過</rb><rt>cʽün-ko</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>*kyiao-leh*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞拿</rb><rt>Üô-nô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞設</rb><rt>Üô-sih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇派</rb><rt>keh-pʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>法內力</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-ne-lih-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>老老了</rb><rt>lao-lao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>新婦</rb><rt>sing-vu</rt><rb>後</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>湊隊</rb><rt>tsʽeo-de</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>毛</rb><rt>mao</rt><rb>八十</rb><rt>pah-jih</rt><rb>四</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日夜</rb><rt>nyih-yia</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謝謝</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。

<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>*Iah-seh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>*Mô-li-üô*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>做好</rb><rt>tso-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>省</rb><rt>*sang*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>大起來</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靈性</rb><rt>ling-sing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>足足個了</rb><rt>tsoh-tsoh-go-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>每年</rb><rt>ME-NYIN</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>逾越節</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>節肯個</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng-go</rt><rb>規矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>滿了</rb><rt>mun-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>Siao-nying</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>耽擱間</rb><rt>tæn-kôh-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>Tsih</rt><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>走勒</rb><rt>tseo-leh</rt><rb>一日個</rb><rt>ih-nyih-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>親眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>熟人</rb><rt>joh-nying</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>尋弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>倒走轉</rb><rt>tao-tseo-cün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 8. 耶（穌）後（頭）8（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>過勒</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>尋着了</rb><rt>zing-djôh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一面</rb><rt>ih-min</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一面</rb><rt>ih-min</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>Tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>答應個</rb><rt>teh-ing-go</rt><rb>靈巧</rb><rt>*ling-kyʽiao*</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>大人</rb><rt>*Do-nying*</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>小人</rb><rt>Siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>尋勒</rb><rt>zing-leh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>傷心</rb><rt>sông-sing</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>。」<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尋</rb><rt>Zing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？」<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>51</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>記勒</rb><rt>kyi-leh</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>大起來</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>越弄</rb><rt>yüih-long</rt><rb>越</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, nying do-kyʽi-læ. 或話，人大起來。（原註）</sup>

### Lk. III.

<u><ruby><rb>該撒</rb><rt>KÆ-SAH</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>提庇留</rb><rt>Di-pi-liu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>第十五</rb><rt>di-jih-ng</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，（<u><ruby><rb>本丟</rb><rt>Peng-tiu</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>猶太</u>個</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa-go</rt><rb>總督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>加利利</u>個</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li-go</rt><rb>藩王</rb><rt>væn-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以土利亞</rb><rt>Yi-tʽu-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>特拉可尼提</rb><rt>Deh-lah-kʽo-nyi-di</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>藩王</rb><rt>væn-wông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>呂撒聶</rb><rt>Li-sah-nyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞比利尼</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pi-li-nyi-go</rt><rb>藩王</rb><rt>væn-wông</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞拿</rb><rt>Üô-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該亞法</rb><rt>Kæ-üô-fah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>撒迦利亞</u>個</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>號令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>四凸亂圈</rb><rt>s-deh-lön-kyʽün</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>回心轉意個</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以賽亞</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üô-go</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>曠野</rb><rt>Kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鋪直</rb><rt>pʽu-dzih</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>街道</rb><rt>ka-dao</rt></ruby>。』<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>山裏個</rb><rt>Sæn-li-go</rt><rb>嶴</rb><rt>ao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>填勒</rb><rt>din-leh</rt><rb>滿</rb><rt>mun</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>嶺</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>低</rb><rt>ti</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>彎曲個</rb><rt>w̆æn-kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>蹊蹺個</rb><rt>kyʽi-kyʽiao-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>平</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>血氣</rb><rt>hyüih-kyʽi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>救星</rb><rt>kyiu-sing</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>*Iah-ʽen*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>毒蛇個</rb><rt>Doh-dzô-go</rt><rb>種黨</rb><rt>cong-tông</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>指引</rb><rt>ts-ying</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>躲避</rb><rt>to-bi</rt><rb>將來個</rb><rt>tsiang-læ-go</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>？ <sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>再話</rb><rt>Tsæ-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>斧頭</rb><rt>fu-deo</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>安勒</rb><rt>en-leh</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>根頭</rb><rt>keng-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>斫倒</rb><rt>tsôh-tao</rt><rb>丟勒</rb><rt>tiu-leh</rt><rb>火裏</rb><rt>ho-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>Keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>？」 <sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>兩件</rb><rt>liang-gyin</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>一件</rb><rt>ih-gyin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>收錢糧</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>？」 <sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>額外</rb><rt>Ngah-nga</rt><rb>錢糧</rb><rt>zin-liang</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>喫錢糧</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>*Iah-ʽen*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>把勢</rb><rt>pô-shü</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>；<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>謊告</rb><rt>hwông-kao</rt><rb>索詐</rb><rt>soh-tsô</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>門份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt><rb>錢糧</rb><rt>zin-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>知足</rb><rt>cü-tsoh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, M-nao hah nying-kô. 或話，嘸得好嚇人家。（原註）</sup>

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>*Kyi-toh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>來其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>鞋帶</rb><rt>ʽa-ta</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>糞箕</rb><rt>feng-kyi</rt><rb>馱的</rb><rt>do-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>揚</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>乾淨</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>曬場裏個</rb><rt>sa-dziang-li-go</rt><rb>穀</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>穀</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>收進</rb><rt>siu-tsing</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>倉裏</rb><rt>tsʽông-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>糠驗</rb><rt>kʽông-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>熓</rb><rt>u</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>燒掉</rb><rt>siao-diao</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>*Iah-ʽen*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>別樣</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>勸化</rb><rt>kyʽün-hwô</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, nyi-ʽôh-peh-kyüih. 或話，疑惑不決。（原註）</sup>

<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>藩王</rb><rt>væn-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>腓力</u>個</rb><rt>Fi-lih-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希羅底</u>個</rb><rt>Hyi-lo-ti-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>做過個</rb><rt>tso-ko-go</rt><rb>壞處</rb><rt>w̆a-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>責罰</rb><rt>tsah-vah</rt></ruby>；<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>加湊</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>關</rb><rt>kwæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>牢監裏</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 30. 耶（穌）後（頭）30（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>PAH-SING</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>受過</rb><rt>ziu-ko</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>開開</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>現出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>鵓鴿</rb><rt>beh-keh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天裏</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>親愛個</rb><rt>tsʽing-æ-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>得意個</rb><rt>teh-i-go</rt></ruby>。」

<u><ruby><rb>拉麥</rb><rt>*Lah-mah*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪土撒拉</rb><rt>Mô-tʽu-sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>瑪土撒拉</rb><rt>*Mô-tʽu-sah-lah*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以諾</rb><rt>Yi-nôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以諾</rb><rt>*Yi-nôh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅列</rb><rt>Yüô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>雅列</rb><rt>*Yüô-lih*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪勒列</rb><rt>Mô-leh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>瑪勒列</rb><rt>*Mô-leh-lih*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該南</rb><rt>Kæ-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby>，<sup>38</sup>
