### Lk. IV.

<sup>1</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>受勒</rb><rt>ziu-leh</rt><rb>滿足</rb><rt>mun-tsoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>領勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>mo-kwe</rt><rb>試試</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>喫過</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>滿了</rb><rt>mun-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>肚飢了</rb><rt>du-kyi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>Mo-kwe</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>變</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>句打句</rb><rt>kyü-tang-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。』 」

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>Mo-kwe</rt><rb>領勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>高高</rb><rt>kao-kao</rt><rb>山頂</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一霎時</rb><rt>ih-sah-z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>萬國</rb><rt>væn-koh</rt><rb>點</rb><rt>tin</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>萬國個</rb><rt>væn-koh-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>拜倒</rb><rt>pa-tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>當該</rb><rt>Tông-kæ</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>獨門</rb><rt>doh-meng</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。』 」

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* *mo-kwe* *ming-z*. 就是魔鬼名字。（原註）</sup>

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>*Mo-kwe*</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿個</rb><rt>sing-din-go</rt><rb>屋腦尖</rb><rt>oh-nao-tsin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>跳落去</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>：<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>保護</rb><rt>pao-wu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>擋</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>bang-djôh</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>。』 」<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>試試</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。』 」

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>Mo-kwe</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>試完</rb><rt>s-wun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>暫時</rb><rt>dzæn-z</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>受勒</rb><rt>ziu-leh</rt><rb>聖靈個</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling-go</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名聲</rb><rt>ming-sing</rt><rb>揚開</rb><rt>yiang-kʽæ</rt><rb>四凸亂圈</rb><rt>s-deh-lön-kyʽün</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 30. 耶（穌）後（頭）30（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>*Yiu*</rt><rb>一遭</rb><rt>*ih-tsao*</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>養大個</rb><rt>iang-do-go</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>閒早</rb><rt>ʽæn-tsao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立起來</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以賽亞</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üô-go</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>書</rb><rt>Shü</rt><rb>攤開</rb><rt>tʽæn-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>翻</rb><rt>fæn</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>18</sup> 「<ruby><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>傅</rb><rt>fu</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>封</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>窮人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>傷心個</rb><rt>sông-sing-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>擄去間</rb><rt>lo-kyʽi-kæn</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>放轉來其</rb><rt>fông-cün-læ-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見其</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>傷害</rb><rt>sông-ʽæ</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>饒放</rb><rt>nyiao-fông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>開恩個</rb><rt>kʽæ-eng-go</rt><rb>年份</rb><rt>nyin-veng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>收攏</rb><rt>siu-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>辦事人</rb><rt>bæn-z-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>聚會堂裏個</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li-go</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇篇</rb><rt>Keh-pʽin</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>Shü</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效驗</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 31. 耶（穌）後（頭）31（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>講出來個</rb><rt>kông-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約瑟</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-seh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>俗話</rb><rt>dzoh-wô</rt><rb>批評</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>醫生</rb><rt>I-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>*Ng*</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦百農</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-nong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>做做</rb><rt>tso-tso</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>先知人</rb><rt>Sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>實惠</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以利亞</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>旱勒</rb><rt>ʽen-leh</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>零</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>落個</rb><rt>loh-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各到處</rb><rt>kôh-tao-cʽü</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>*tsʽa*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西頓</rb><rt>Si-teng</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>撒勒大</rb><rt>Sah-leh-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>以利沙</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>大麻風</rb><rt>da-mô-fong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>乾淨</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>敘利亞</rb><rt>Jü-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>乃幔</rb><rt>Næ-mæn</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>*i*</rt><rb>乾淨了</rb><rt>*ken-zing-de*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>Jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>，<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>城外</rb><rt>zing-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拉</rb><rt>l̆a</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>山巖</rb><rt>sæn-ngæn</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>建間個</rb><rt>kyin-kæn-go</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>）<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>推</rb><rt>tʽe</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>落去</rb><rt>lôh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦百農</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-nong</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安息</rb><rt>en-sih</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>Tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>Jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼個</rb><rt>kyü-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>附的了</rb><rt>vu-tih-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>胡嚨</rb><rt>wu-long</rt><rb>響亮</rb><rt>hyiang-liang</rt><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>34</sup> 「<ruby><rb>哎</rb><rt>ʽE</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb><u>拿撒勒</u>個</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>相干</rb><rt>siang-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>狠</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>甮</rb><rt>Vong</rt><rb>響</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>鬼</rb><rt>Kyü</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出去了</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>傷</rb><rt>sông</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>講來講去</rb><rt>kông-læ-kông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鬼</rb><rt>*kyü*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名聲</rb><rt>ming-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>揚開</rb><rt>yiang-kʽæ</rt><rb>四凸亂圈</rb><rt>s-deh-lön-kyʽün</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立起身</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>聚會堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>*tseo-cʽih*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>西門</u>個</rb><rt>Si-meng-go</rt><rb>丈姆</rb><rt>dziang-m</rt><rb>潮熱</rb><rt>dziao-nyih</rt><rb>發勒</rb><rt>fah-leh</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>身邊</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>責罰</rb><rt>tsah-vah</rt><rb>潮熱</rb><rt>dziao-nyih</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>病</rb><rt>*bing*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>退了</rb><rt>tʽe-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>*nyü-nying*</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>供應</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>日頭</rb><rt>Nyih-deo</rt><rb>落山</rb><rt>lôh-sæn</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>隨便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個打個</rb><rt>ko-tang-ko</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>狠</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>許</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>*kyü*</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尋到</rb><rt>zing-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>留住</rb><rt>liu-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>別開</rb><rt>bih-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>神國個</rb><rt>Jing-koh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>差來個</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。

---

### Lk. V.

<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>YIU</rt><rb>一潮</rb><rt>ih-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>挨攏</rb><rt>a-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立勒</rb><rt>lih-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革尼撒勒</rb><rt>Keh-nyi-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>湖</rb><rt>wu</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>兩隻</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>湖邊</rb><rt>wu-pin</rt><rb>拋間</rb><rt>pʽao-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抲魚個</rb><rt>kʽô-ng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>網</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西門</u>個</rb><rt>Si-meng-go</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>跳落去</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撐勒</rb><rt>tsʽang-leh</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>撐</rb><rt>Tsʽang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>深點</rb><rt>sing-tin</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>網</rb><rt>mông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>落去</rb><rt>lôh-kyʽi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>整夜</rb><rt>tsing-yia</rt><rb>勞碌</rb><rt>lao-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>抲着</rb><rt>kʽô-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>網</rb><rt>mông</rt><rb>下落去</rb><rt>ʽô-lôh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>下落去</rb><rt>ʽô-lôh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攔進</rb><rt>lah-tsing</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有夥</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>網</rb><rt>mông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爆開了</rb><rt>pao-kʽæ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>搖搖</rb><rt>yiao-yiao</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>別隻</rb><rt>bih-tsah</rt><rb>船裏個</rb><rt>jün-li-go</rt><rb>拼夥計</rb><rt>pʽing-ho-kyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>幫襯記</rb><rt>pông-tsʽeng-kyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兩隻</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>*ng*</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>裝滿了</rb><rt>tsông-mun-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>沉落去了</rb><rt>dzing-lôh-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>腳下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>隊夥</rb><rt>de-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一網</rb><rt>ih-mông</rt><rb>抲着個</rb><rt>kʽô-djôh-go</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>喫𢞕了</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb><u>西庇太</u>個</rb><rt>Si-pi-tʽa-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拼夥計個</rb><rt>pʽing-ho-kyi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>*kyʽih-hoh*</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>Hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>從今以後</rb><rt>dzong-kying-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>撐攏</rb><rt>tsʽang-long</rt><rb>岸</rb><rt>ngen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>YIU</rt><rb>一遭</rb><rt>ih-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>渾身</rb><rt>weng-sing</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>大麻風</rb><rt>da-mô-fong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>撲倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>肯個</rb><rt>kʽeng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>乾淨</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>伸出來</rb><rt>sing-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>按</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一按</rb><rt>ih-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>肯個</rb><rt>kʽeng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>乾淨</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>大麻風</rb><rt>Da-mô-fong</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>褪掉了</rb><rt>tʽeng-diao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>乾淨</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>*tsi-veh*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>名聲</rb><rt>ming-sing</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>揚開了</rb><rt>yiang-kʽæ-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聚攏來</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>*kông*</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>*dao-li*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>不常</rb><rt>*peh-djông*</rt><rb>走記過</rb><rt>tseo-kyi-ko</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 走記過：避過</sup>

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>*bông-pin*</rt><rb>坐間</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>鄉村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>風癱</rb><rt>fong-tʽæn</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鋪板</rb><rt>pʽu-pæn</rt><rb>睏仔</rb><rt>kwʽeng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>抬勒來</rb><rt>dæ-leh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>抬進去</rb><rt>dæ-tsing-kyʽi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>忖弗出</rb><rt>tsʽeng-feh-cʽih</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>進去個</rb><rt>tsing-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>fông-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>屋頂</rb><rt>oh-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>瓦片</rb><rt>ngô-pʽin</rt><rb>𢳚開仔</rb><rt>*hyiao-kʽæ-ts*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>鋪板</rb><rt>pʽu-pæn</rt><rb>吊落</rb><rt>tiao-lôh</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>風癱</rb><rt>fong-tʽæn</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾儂</rb><rt>Ng-noh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>赦掉了</rb><rt>sô-diao-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>Doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>*sing-li*</rt><rb>議論起來</rb><rt>nyi-leng-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>褻瀆個</rb><rt>sih-doh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>獨一個</rb><rt>doh-ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>議論</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>議論</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt></ruby>？<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>Wa-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>赦掉了</rb><rt>sô-diao-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>容易</rb><rt>yüong-yi</rt></ruby>？<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>赦罪個</rb><rt>sô-ze-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>風癱</rb><rt>fong-tʽæn</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>鋪板</rb><rt>pʽu-pæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*Keh*</rt><rb>風癱</rb><rt>*fong-tʽæn*</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>*cü-kwu*</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>睏個</rb><rt>kwʽeng-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依路</rb><rt>*i-lu*</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>驚駭</rb><rt>kying-hyiæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>怕起來了</rb><rt>pʽô-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>今末仔</rb><rt>Kyih-mih-ts</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>看見過了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>收錢糧</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>戶糧房</rb><rt>wu-liang-vông</rt><rb>坐間</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>跟</rb><rt>Keng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爬起身</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>一席</rb><rt>ih-zih</rt><rb>大菜</rb><rt>do-tsʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>收錢糧</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>坐席</rb><rt>zo-zih</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>Gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>收錢糧</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>嘸病個</rb><rt>M-bing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>醫生</rb><rt>i-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有病個</rb><rt>yiu-bing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要個</rb><rt>*iao-go*</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>*eo*</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>*we-sing-cün-i*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>約翰</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>不常</rb><rt>peh-djông</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>賀</rb><rt>ʽO</rt><rb>好日</rb><rt>hao-nyih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>新郎</rb><rt>sing-lông</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>將來個</rb><rt>tsiang-læ-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>新郎</rb><rt>sing-lông</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>禁食其</rb><rt>kying-zih-gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>M-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>扯</rb><rt>cʽô</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>補</rb><rt>pu</rt><rb>舊</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>新個</rb><rt>sing-go</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>*pô*</rt><rb>舊個</rb><rt>*gyiu-go*</rt><rb>扯開</rb><rt>cʽô-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再話</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>補的</rb><rt>pu-tih</rt><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>新個</rb><rt>sing-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>舊個</rb><rt>gyiu-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相配</rb><rt>siang-pʽe</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>舊</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>皮袋</rb><rt>bi-dæ</rt><rb>𣥼</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>爆開</rb><rt>pao-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>皮袋</rb><rt>bi-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>倒掉</rb><rt>tao-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>皮袋</rb><rt>bi-dæ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弄環</rb><rt>long-wæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>Tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>皮袋</rb><rt>bi-dæ</rt><rb>𣥼</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>兩樣</rb><rt>liang-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>保全了</rb><rt>pao-djün-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>M-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>老</rb><rt>lao</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>喫過</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>*kyʽüoh*</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>*tsiu*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>老個</rb><rt>lao-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。』」

### Lk. VI.

<ruby><rb>無酵節</rb><rt>*VU-KAO-TSIH*</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>第一個</rb><rt>di-ih-go</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>麥田裏</rb><rt>mah-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>摘勒</rb><rt>tsah-leh</rt><rb>麥佩</rb><rt>mah-be</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>搓搓</rb><rt>tsʽo-tsʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>安息日</rb><rt>En-sih-nyih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>？」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>肚飢</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>讀過</rb><rt>doh-ko</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>擺間個</rb><rt>pa-kæn-go</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>馱來</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>安息日個</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>順隻</rb><rt>jing-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>風了</rb><rt>fong-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>Doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>偷伴</rb><rt>tʽeo-bun</rt><rb>張東</rb><rt>tsiang-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>因頭</rb><rt>ing-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>風手</rb><rt>fong-siu</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爬起</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt><rb>立東了</rb><rt>lih-tong-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>安息</rb><rt>En-sih</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>好事</rb><rt>hao-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>壞事</rb><rt>w̆a-z</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喪</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>*sing-ming*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>作興</rb><rt>tsôh-hying</rt></ruby>？」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>團圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>伸帶出來</rb><rt>sing-tæn-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伸出來</rb><rt>sing-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好了</rb><rt>hao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>別隻</rb><rt>bih-tsah</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>怏勒</rb><rt>ông-leh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>顛癡癡</rb><rt>tin-cʽü-cʽü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>KEH-GO</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>山裏</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>整夜</rb><rt>tsing-yia</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>謳攏來</rb><rt>eo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>挑選</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün</rt><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，（<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，）<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安得烈</rb><rt>En-teh-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>*wa-yiu*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴多羅買</rb><rt>Pô-to-lo-mæ</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>馬太</rb><rt>Mô-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>多馬</rb><rt>To-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb><u>亞勒腓</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-leh-fi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>銳羅底</rb><rt>Ze-lo-ti</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>*hyüong-di*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加略</rb><rt>Kyüô-liah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>*ma-diao*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>*sæn*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>平地裏</rb><rt>bing-di-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>*keh-deo*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>各到處</rb><rt>kôh-tao-cʽü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>推羅</rb><rt>Tʽe-lo</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>西頓</rb><rt>Si-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>*kông*</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>*dao-li*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>試</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>磨難</rb><rt>mo-næn</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>Keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>按</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一按</rb><rt>ih-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>醫好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬帶起</rb><rt>dæ-tæn-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>*NG-LAH*</rt><rb>窮個</rb><rt>gyüong-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神國</rb><rt>Jing-koh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>*Ng-lah*</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>肚飢</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>喫飽</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao</rt></ruby>。

「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>*Ng-lah*</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>哭個</rb><rt>kʽoh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>發笑</rb><rt>fah-siao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>惡愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𧮙罵</rb><rt>zoh-mô</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>名聲</rb><rt>ming-sing</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>臭臭</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽiu</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>*tong-si*</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>丟掉個</rb><rt>tiu-diao-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歡歡喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt><rb>跳跳</rb><rt>tʽiao-tʽiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>報應</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt><rb>大勒緊</rb><rt>do-leh-kying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> 「<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>安耽</rb><rt>en-tæn</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>得着過了</rb><rt>teh-djôh-ko-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>喫飽</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>肚飢</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt></ruby>。

「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>笑</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>悲傷</rb><rt>pe-shông</rt><rb>啼哭</rb><rt>di-kʽoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> 「<ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>咬</rb><rt>ngao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>NG-LAH</rt><rb>聽東個</rb><rt>tʽing-tong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>冤家</rb><rt>ün-kô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>惡愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>；<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>咒罵</rb><rt>tsiu-mô</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弄送</rb><rt>long-song</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>刮</rb><rt>kwah</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>蕩邊</rb><rt>*dông-pin*</rt><rb>巴掌</rb><rt>pô-công</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇邊</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>刮</rb><rt>kwah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>外向</rb><rt>nga-hyiang</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>剝勒去</rb><rt>poh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>討</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>奪勒去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>討</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>去惜</rb><rt>kyʽi-sih</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>待</rb><rt>Dæ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>借</rb><rt>tsia</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>照數</rb><rt>tsiao-su</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>借</rb><rt>tsia</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>冤家</rb><rt>ün-kô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>借</rb><rt>tsia</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>報應</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>忘恩個</rb><rt>vông-eng-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>惡人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>好心</rb><rt>hao-sing</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>哀憐心</rb><rt>æ-lin-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>哀憐心</rb><rt>æ-lin-sing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>批評</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>批評</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>原諒</rb><rt>nyün-liang</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>原諒</rb><rt>nyün-liang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>斗桶</rb><rt>teo-dong</rt><rb>量足</rb><rt>liang-tsoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扤過仔</rb><rt>ngah-ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撳</rb><rt>kyʽing</rt><rb>結實仔</rb><rt>kyih-jih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>零星</rb><rt>ling-sing</rt><rb>溢出</rb><rt>kah-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>倒勒</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>兜裏</rb><rt>teo-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>量</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>量</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>Hah-ts</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>兩下</rb><rt>liang-ʽô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>窞裏</rb><rt>den-li</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>學生子</rb><rt>ʽÔh-sang-ts</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>過於</rb><rt>ko-ü</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>全備</rb><rt>djün-be</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>看出</rb><rt>kʽen-cʽih</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>灰塵</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>忖到</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>棟樑</rb><rt>tong-liang</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>？<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>眼睛裏</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing-li</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>灰塵</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>挔出仔</rb><rt>i-cʽih-ts</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>爾自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>眼睛裏</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing-li</rt><rb>箇根</rb><rt>keh-keng</rt><rb>棟樑</rb><rt>tong-liang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>看弗出</rb><rt>kʽen-feh-cʽih</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>假好人</rb><rt>Kô-hao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>挔出</rb><rt>i-cʽih</rt><rb>爾自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>眼睛裏個</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing-li-go</rt><rb>棟樑</rb><rt>tong-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>挔出</rb><rt>i-cʽih</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>眼睛裏個</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing-li-go</rt><rb>灰塵</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>43</sup> 「<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>Sang</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>株打株</rb><rt>Cü-tang-cü</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>認勒出</rb><rt>nying-leh-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>刺棚裏</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>無花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>野草</rb><rt>yia-tsʽao</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>摘</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>紫葡萄</rb><rt>ts-bu-dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>好人</rb><rt>Hao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>囥的個</rb><rt>kʽông-tih-go</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>發出</rb><rt>fah-cʽih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>壞人</rb><rt>w̆a-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>囥的個</rb><rt>kʽông-tih-go</rt><rb>壞處</rb><rt>w̆a-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>發出</rb><rt>fah-cʽih</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt><rb>講出來</rb><rt>kông-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>溢出來個</rb><rt>kah-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>多頭</rb><rt>to-deo</rt></ruby>。

「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>？<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>可比像</rb><rt>kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>Ziang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>oh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爛泥</rb><rt>*na-nyi*</rt><rb>掘勒</rb><rt>gyüih-leh</rt><rb>深深</rb><rt>sing-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>屋裏個</rb><rt>*oh-li-go*</rt><rb>牆腳</rb><rt>ziang-kyiah</rt><rb>放勒</rb><rt>fông-leh</rt><rb>石頭上</rb><rt>zah-deo-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>大水</rb><rt>do-shü</rt><rb>發作</rb><rt>fah-tsôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>上落水旺</rb><rt>zông-lôh-shü-wông</rt><rb>沖着</rb><rt>cʽong-djôh</rt><rb>箇進</rb><rt>keh-tsing</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>oh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>搖動</rb><rt>yiao-dong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>牆腳</rb><rt>ziang-kyiah</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>石頭上</rb><rt>zah-deo-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>聽聞仔</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ts</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可比像</rb><rt>kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>牆腳</rb><rt>ziang-kyiah</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>oh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>泥塗頂</rb><rt>nyi-du-teng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>上落水旺</rb><rt>zông-lôh-shü-wông</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>沖着</rb><rt>cʽong-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇進</rb><rt>keh-tsing</rt><rb>屋個</rb><rt>oh-go</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>來勒</rb><rt>læ-leh</rt><rb>厲害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Lk. VII.

<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>完了</rb><rt>wun-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦百農</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-nong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城裏</rb><rt>*zing-li*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>百總個</rb><rt>pah-tsong-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>中意個</rb><rt>cong-i-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>Tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>風聲</rb><rt>fong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>*kyi-go*</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>血心</rb><rt>hyüih-sing</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>*eng-we*</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>受個</rb><rt>ziu-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聚會堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>有限</rb><rt>yiu-ʽæn</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百總</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>勞步</rb><rt>lao-bu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>舍邊</rb><rt>sô-pin</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>敢當</rb><rt>ken-tông</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>只消</rb><rt>tsih-siao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>傭人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>好其</rb><rt>hao-gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup>
