### Lk. XII.

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾萬</rb><rt>kyi-væn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聚攏來</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>踔來踔去</rb><rt>nao-læ-nao-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>酵水</rb><rt>kao-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>假好</rb><rt>kô-hao</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>暗藏</rb><rt>En-dzông</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>*m-yiu*</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>*ih-yiang*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>露出來</rb><rt>lu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>暗暗</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>亮亮</rb><rt>liang-liang</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>密弗洞風</rb><rt>mih-feh-dong-fong</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>張勒</rb><rt>tsiang-leh</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋頂</rb><rt>oh-teng</rt><rb>傳開去</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>*we*</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>別樣</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>地獄裏</rb><rt>di-nyüoh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>五隻</rb><rt>Ng-tsah</rt><rb>麻雀</rb><rt>mô-tsiang</rt><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>二分</rb><rt>nyi-feng</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>買了</rb><rt>ma-de</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其中</rb><rt>gyi-cong</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>忘記</rb><rt>mông-kyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>根根</rb><rt>keng-keng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>數好了</rb><rt>shü-hao-liao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>麻雀</rb><rt>mô-tsiang</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>貴重</rb><rt>kwe-djong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赦</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>掉</rb><rt>diao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>褻瀆</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>赦弗掉個</rb><rt>sô-feh-diao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>Nying-kô</rt><rb>拖</rb><rt>tʽo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>愁</rb><rt>zeo</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>樣式</rb><rt>yiang-shih</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>分剖</rb><rt>feng-pʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>當時</rb><rt>tông-z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt><rb>遺落個</rb><rt>yi-lôh-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>分分開</rb><rt>feng-feng-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>封</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>審</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>官司</rb><rt>kwun-s</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>家計</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>謹防</rb><rt>kying-bông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>貪心</rb><rt>tʽen-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>家計</rb><rt>*kô-kyi*</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>家計</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>田地裏個</rb><rt>din-di-li-go</rt><rb>年成</rb><rt>nyin-dzing</rt><rb>好勒緊</rb><rt>hao-leh-kying</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>出產</rb><rt>cʽih-tsʽæn</rt><rb>嘸采</rb><rt>m-tsʽæ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>囥</rb><rt>kʽông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>弄法</rb><rt>long-fah</rt></ruby>？』<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>穀倉</rb><rt>koh-tsʽông</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>拆掉仔</rb><rt>tsʽah-diao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>大點</rb><rt>do-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>出產</rb><rt>cʽih-tsʽæn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>貨物</rb><rt>ho-veh</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>囥</rb><rt>kʽông</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>活靈</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>活靈</rb><rt>Weh-ling</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>貨物</rb><rt>ho-veh</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有夥</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt><rb>年數</rb><rt>nyin-su</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>安心</rb><rt>en-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喫喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>喝喝</rb><rt>hah-hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>快活</rb><rt>kwʽa-weh</rt></ruby>。』<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>愚笨個</rb><rt>Nyü-beng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>討</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>活靈</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>備好間個</rb><rt>be-hao-kæn-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？』<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>積聚</rb><rt>tsih-jü</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇株</rb><rt>keh-cü</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>愁悶</rb><rt>zeo-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>愁悶</rb><rt>*zeo-meng*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <sup>29</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>腰縛裏</rb><rt>iao-bo-li</rt><rb>繳身帶</rb><rt>kyiao-sing-ta</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>繫好東</rb><rt>kyi-hao-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>燈盞</rb><rt>teng-tsæn</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>點好東</rb><rt>tin-hao-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>太平</rb><rt>tʽa-bing</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>造開</rb><rt>zao-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>阿婆</rb><rt>ah-bo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>新婦</rb><rt>sing-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>新婦</rb><rt>sing-vu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿婆</rb><rt>ah-bo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>造開</rb><rt>zao-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>54</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一朵</rb><rt>ih-tô</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>西半邊</rb><rt>si-pun-pin</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馬上</rb><rt>mô-zông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>落雨了</rb><rt>lôh-yü-de</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>雨</rb><rt>yü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>落了</rb><rt>lôh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>55</sup> <ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>*Kʽen-kyin*</rt><rb>發</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>南風</rb><rt>nen-fong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>天家</rb><rt>Tʽin-kô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>熱了</rb><rt>nyih-de</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>*ʽeo-deo*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>熱了</rb><rt>nyih-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>假好人</rb><rt>Kô-hao-nying</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看勒出</rb><rt>kʽen-leh-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>看弗出</rb><rt>kʽen-feh-cʽih</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>57</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>*dao-li*</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>辨</rb><rt>bin</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>清通</rb><rt>tsʽing-tʽong</rt></ruby>？<sup>58</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>對頭</rb><rt>te-deo</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>半路裏</rb><rt>pun-lu-li</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>上緊</rb><rt>zông-kying</rt><rb>打磨</rb><rt>tang-mo</rt><rb>脫出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>拖</rb><rt>tʽo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>皂吏</rb><rt>zao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>皂吏</rb><rt>zao-li</rt><rb>挈</rb><rt>kyʽih</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>牢監裏</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>59</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>煞腳個</rb><rt>Sah-kyiah-go</rt><rb>銅鈿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>還完</rb><rt>wæn-wun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>牢監裏</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Lk. XIII.

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>*kyi-go*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>和攏了</rb><rt>ʽo-long-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djôh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>*ʽo-se*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>格外</rb><rt>kah-nga</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>十八個</rb><rt>jih-pah-go</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西羅亞</rb><rt>Si-lo-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>高樓</rb><rt>kao-leo</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>壓煞個</rb><rt>ah-sah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>住</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>格外</rb><rt>kah-nga</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Peng-veng* *z-ngæn*, kyʽin tsa. 本文字眼，欠債。（原註）</sup>

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>無花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>葡萄園裏</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün-li</rt><rb>種間</rb><rt>cong-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尋尋</rb><rt>*zing-zing*</rt><rb>尋弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>園頭</rb><rt>yün-deo</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇株</rb><rt>Keh-cü</rt><rb>無花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尋尋</rb><rt>*zing-zing*</rt><rb>尋弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>斫掉</rb><rt>tsôh-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>討力</rb><rt>tʽao-lih</rt><rb>何用</rb><rt>ʽo-yüong</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？』<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>園頭</rb><rt>Yün-deo</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今年</rb><rt>kying-nyin</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>團團</rb><rt>dön-dön</rt><rb>掘</rb><rt>gyüih</rt><rb>一掘</rb><rt>ih-gyüih</rt><rb>壅壅</rb><rt>üong-üong</rt><rb>糞</rb><rt>feng</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>*ziu*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>*yi*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>斫</rb><rt>tsôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>掉</rb><rt>diao</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>安息</rb><rt>EN-SIH</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>風病</rb><rt>fong-bing</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>十八</rb><rt>jih-pah</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>腰骨</rb><rt>iao-kweh</rt><rb>藃攏</rb><rt>ho-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>直弗起</rb><rt>dzih-feh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳攏來</rb><rt>eo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>脫掉了</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>兩隻</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一按</rb><rt>ih-en</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>*nyü-nying*</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>直起來了</rb><rt>dzih-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>管</rb><rt>Kwun</rt><rb>聚會堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>恨</rb><rt>ʽeng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>六</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>六</rb><rt>*loh*</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>*nyih*</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>假好人</rb><rt>Kô-hao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>一般</rb><rt>ih-pæn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>圈裏</rb><rt>gyin-li</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牽出</rb><rt>kyʽin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>況且</rb><rt>Hwông-tsʽia</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>縛住</rb><rt>bo-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>十八</rb><rt>jih-pah</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>對頭</rb><rt>te-deo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>惶恐了</rb><rt>wông-kʽong-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做出來個</rb><rt>tso-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>有夥</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt><rb>光明正大</rb><rt>kwông-ming-tsing-da</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>歡喜了</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>可比像</rb><rt>Kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>一粒</rb><rt>ih-lih</rt><rb>芥菜子</rb><rt>ka-tsʽæ-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>馱仔</rb><rt>do-ts</rt><rb>下</rb><rt>ʽô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>花園裏</rb><rt>hwô-yün-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>長起來</rb><rt>dziang-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>成勒</rb><rt>dzing-leh</rt><rb>一株</rb><rt>ih-cü</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天裏個</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>鳥</rb><rt>tiao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>庉勒</rb><rt>deng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>椏枝裏</rb><rt>ô-ts-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>可比像</rb><rt>Kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>酵水</rb><rt>kao-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>馱來</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>囥勒</rb><rt>kʽông-leh</rt><rb>三斗</rb><rt>sæn-teo</rt><rb>麪粉</rb><rt>min-feng</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>發酵</rb><rt>fah-kao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>順埭</rb><rt>jing-da</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>鄉村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>24</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拼命</rb><rt>pʽing-ming</rt><rb>撐進</rb><rt>tsʽang-tsing</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一頭</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt><rb>狹窄個</rb><rt>ʽah-tsah-go</rt><rb>門裏</rb><rt>meng-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>走進去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>Cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>關好</rb><rt>kwæn-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>立勒</rb><rt>lih-leh</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>敲敲</rb><rt>kʽao-kʽao</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>開開</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽæ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，』<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>』——<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>喫過了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ko-de</rt><rb>喝過了</rb><rt>hah-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>大街上</rb><rt>do-ka-zông</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。』<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>非禮</rb><rt>fi-li</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>走開去</rb><rt>tseo-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神國</rb><rt>Jing-koh</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>趕出東</rb><rt>ken-cʽih-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>咬勒</rb><rt>ngao-leh</rt><rb>牙齒</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt><rb>啼哭個</rb><rt>di-kʽoh-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>東邊</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>西邊</rb><rt>si-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>南邊</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>北邊</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神國裏</rb><rt>Jing-koh-li</rt><rb>坐席</rb><rt>zo-zih</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>在後個</rb><rt>dzæ-ʽeo-go</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>上前</rb><rt>zông-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>在前個</rb><rt>dzæ-zin-go</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>落後</rb><rt>lôh-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>別</rb><rt>bih</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打賬</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>走去</rb><rt>tseo-kyʽi</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>狐狸</rb><rt>wu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>病痛</rb><rt>bing-tʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>全備了</rb><rt>djün-be-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>後日</rb><rt>ʽeo-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>走個</rb><rt>tseo-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>弄煞個</rb><rt>long-sah-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>！<u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>！<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞煞</rb><rt>kʽang-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>回數</rb><rt>we-su</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>聚</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>攏來</rb><rt>long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>雞娘</rb><rt>kyi-nyiang</rt><rb>翼梢</rb><rt>yiah-sao</rt><rb>底下</rb><rt>ti-ʽô</rt><rb>捂</rb><rt>*u*</rt><rb>小雞</rb><rt>siao-kyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt></ruby>！<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>變勒</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>空地</rb><rt>kʽong-di</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>Kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是應該</rb><rt>z-ing-kæ</rt><rb>祝讚個</rb><rt>coh-tsæn-go</rt></ruby>』<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。」

### Lk. XIV.

<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>YIU</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>頭腦拉</rb><rt>deo-nao-lah</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>喫飯</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-væn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>偷伴</rb><rt>tʽeo-bun</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>東</rb><rt>tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>當</rb><rt>Tông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>臌脹</rb><rt>kwu-tsiang</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>安息日</rb><rt>En-sih-nyih</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>好使得</rb><rt>hao-s-teh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>響</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>給</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>醫好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一匹</rb><rt>ih-pʽih</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>一頭</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>地坑裏</rb><rt>di-kʽang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>撩</rb><rt>liao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上來</rb><rt>zông-læ</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>應弗來</rb><rt>ing-feh-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>請間個</rb><rt>tsʽing-kæn-go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>揀</rb><rt>kæn</rt><rb>頭位</rb><rt>deo-we</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>8</sup> 「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>好日酒</rb><rt>hao-nyih-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>頭位</rb><rt>deo-we</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>請間個</rb><rt>tsʽing-kæn-go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>尊重個</rb><rt>tseng-djong-go</rt></ruby>，<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>讓</rb><rt>nyiang</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>坐仔</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>惶恐相</rb><rt>wông-kʽong-siang</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>結煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>請去個</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>結煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>朋友</rb><rt>Beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>坐仔</rb><rt>*zo-ts*</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>坐客</rb><rt>zo-kʽah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>體面</rb><rt>tʽi-min</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>降</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>低</rb><rt>ti</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>低</rb><rt>ti</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>升</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>備</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>晝飯</rb><rt>tsiu-væn</rt><rb>備</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>夜飯</rb><rt>yia-væn</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>親眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>銅鈿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>鄰舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾儂</rb><rt>ng-noh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>報應</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt><rb>得着了</rb><rt>teh-djôh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>下飯</rb><rt>ʽô-væn</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>窮苦</rb><rt>gyüong-kwʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>殘疾</rb><rt>dzæn-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拐腳</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>禮</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>報應</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>坐客</rb><rt>Zo-kʽah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>神國裏</rb><rt>Jing-koh-li</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>飯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>辦了</rb><rt>bæn-leh</rt><rb>一席</rb><rt>ih-zih</rt><rb>大酒</rb><rt>do-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>坐席個</rb><rt>zo-zih-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>請間個</rb><rt>tsʽing-kæn-go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百樣</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>備好了</rb><rt>be-hao-de</rt></ruby>。』<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>*sing-siang*</rt><rb>回覆了</rb><rt>we-foh-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>頭一個</rb><rt>Deo-ih-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一畈</rb><rt>ih-pæn</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>買間</rb><rt>ma-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>務必子</rb><rt>vu-pih-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看過個</rb><rt>kʽen-ko-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>原諒</rb><rt>nyün-liang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。』<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十頭</rb><rt>jih-deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>買間</rb><rt>ma-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>試試看</rb><rt>s-s-kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>原諒</rb><rt>nyün-liang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。』<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>剛剛</rb><rt>*kông-kông*</rt><rb>好日過</rb><rt>hao-nyih-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。』<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>走歸</rb><rt>tseo-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>Cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>Kwʽa</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>城裏個</rb><rt>zing-li-go</rt><rb>街街弄弄</rb><rt>ka-ka-long-long</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>窮苦</rb><rt>gyüong-kwʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>殘疾</rb><rt>dzæn-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拐腳</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>領勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。』<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>Nu-boh</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>做好了</rb><rt>tso-hao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>空個</rb><rt>kʽong-go</rt><rb>坐頭</rb><rt>zo-deo</rt></ruby>。』<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>Cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>大路上</rb><rt>do-lu-zông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>槍笆沿</rb><rt>tsʽiang-pô-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>*keh-deo*</rt><rb>來間個</rb><rt>*læ-kæn-go*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>硬抲</rb><rt>ngang-kʽô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>進來</rb><rt>tsing-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>坐滿</rb><rt>zo-mun</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>Keh-pæn</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>請間個</rb><rt>tsʽing-kæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>下飯個</rb><rt>ʽô-væn-go</rt><rb>味道</rb><rt>mi-dao</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嚐嚐看</rb><rt>zông-zông-kʽen</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>YIU</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>扭轉</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>26</sup> 「<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>爹孃</rb><rt>tia-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老婆</rb><rt>lao-bo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>看輕</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>揹</rb><rt>pe</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>高樓</rb><rt>kao-leo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>坐帶落</rb><rt>zo-tæn-lôh</rt><rb>派</rb><rt>pʽa</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>費用</rb><rt>fi-yüong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來東個</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt><rb>銅鈿</rb><rt>*dong-din*</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>完工</rb><rt>wun-kong</rt><rb>夠了</rb><rt>keo-de</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>Sæn-leh</rt><rb>地盤</rb><rt>di-bun</rt><rb>安好仔</rb><rt>en-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>完工弗來</rb><rt>wun-kong-feh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>*ih*</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>*kʽen*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>取笑</rb><rt>cʽü-siao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>30</sup> 『<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>造造</rb><rt>zao-zao</rt><rb>高樓</rb><rt>*kao-leo*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>*tsih-yiu*</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>完工</rb><rt>wun-kong</rt></ruby>。』<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽÔh-tsia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>國王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>國王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>坐帶落</rb><rt>zo-tæn-lôh</rt><rb>商量好</rb><rt>siang-liang-hao</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>一萬</rb><rt>ih-væn</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抵敵</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>二萬</rb><rt>nyi-væn</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>打勒</rb><rt>tang-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？』<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>趁</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>遠遠</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>求和</rb><rt>gyiu-ʽo</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>是介個</rb><rt>Z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> 「<ruby><rb>鹽</rb><rt>Yin</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>鹽</rb><rt>yin</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>失掉</rb><rt>shih-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>鹹個</rb><rt>ʽæn-go</rt><rb>味道</rb><rt>mi-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>鹹</rb><rt>ʽæn</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>純</rb><rt>*Jing*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>壅</rb><rt>üong</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>嘸做</rb><rt>m-tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>和勒</rb><rt>ʽo-leh</rt><rb>肥糞</rb><rt>bi-feng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸做</rb><rt>m-tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>倒勒</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Lk. XV.

<ruby><rb>收錢糧</rb><rt>SIU-ZIN-LIANG</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>收留</rb><rt>siu-liu</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>喫飯</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-væn</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>4</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一百隻</rb><rt>ih-pah-tsah</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>弗見了</rb><rt>feh-kyin-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>九十</rb><rt>kyiu-jih</rt><rb>九</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>隻</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>弗見個</rb><rt>feh-kyin-go</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>着</rb><rt>djôh</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>尋着仔</rb><rt>Zing-djôh-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歡歡喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt><rb>肩胛頭</rb><rt>kyin-kah-deo</rt><rb>揹仔</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>，<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳攏</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鄰舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇隻</rb><rt>keh-tsah</rt><rb>弗見個</rb><rt>feh-kyin-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>尋着了</rb><rt>zing-djôh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>歡喜相</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-siang</rt></ruby>。』<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>九十</rb><rt>kyiu-jih</rt><rb>九</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>*wa*</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>*yü-kô*</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>*hwun-hyi*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> 「<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽOh-tsia</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十塊</rb><rt>jih-kwʽe</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>弗見了</rb><rt>feh-kyin-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>點勒</rb><rt>tin-leh</rt><rb>蠟燭</rb><rt>lah-coh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>掃掃</rb><rt>sao-sao</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仔仔細細</rb><rt>ts-ts-si-si</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>着</rb><rt>djôh</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>尋着仔</rb><rt>Zing-djôh-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳攏</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>女人隊</rb><rt>nyü-nying-de</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>鄰舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>Keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>弗見個</rb><rt>feh-kyin-go</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>尋着了</rb><rt>zing-djôh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>歡喜相</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-siang</rt></ruby>。』<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Dông-deo* *wô* jih-kwʽe nying-ts, *me-kwʽe* *ih-din* *djong*. 蕩頭話十塊銀子，每塊一鈿重。（原註）</sup>

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>一份</rb><rt>ih-veng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇份</rb><rt>*keh-veng*</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>田頭</rb><rt>din-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>泥豬</rb><rt>nyi-cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>路</rb><rt>Lu</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>有夥</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>看見了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>發</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>慈悲心</rb><rt>dz-pe-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奔上去</rb><rt>peng-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>捼勒</rb><rt>o-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>頭頸</rb><rt>deo-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嗅嗅嘴</rb><rt>hyüong-hyüong-cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup>
