### Lk. XVI.

<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>一份</rb><rt>Ih-veng</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>把賬</rb><rt>pô-tsiang</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>良士</rb><rt>lang-z</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>把賬</rb><rt>Pô-tsiang</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>給</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>家計</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>費掉</rb><rt>fi-diao</rt></ruby>。』<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>良士</rb><rt>*Lang-z*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>把賬</rb><rt>pô-tsiang</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>把賬</rb><rt>pô-tsiang</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>做弗來了</rb><rt>tso-feh-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>經手個</rb><rt>kying-siu-go</rt><rb>賬目</rb><rt>tsiang-moh</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。』<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>把賬</rb><rt>Pô-tsiang</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>把賬個</rb><rt>pô-tsiang-go</rt><rb>缺分</rb><rt>kyʽüih-veng</rt><rb>良士</rb><rt>lang-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>革掉</rb><rt>kah-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>弄法</rb><rt>long-fah</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>耕</rb><rt>kang</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>嘸氣力</rb><rt>m-kyʽi-lih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>討飯</rb><rt>tʽao-væn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>曉得了</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>把賬</rb><rt>pô-tsiang</rt><rb>缺分</rb><rt>kyʽüih-veng</rt><rb>革掉</rb><rt>kah-diao</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>良士</rb><rt>lang-z</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>謳攏來</rb><rt>eo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>頭一個</rb><rt>deo-ih-go</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>良士</rb><rt>lang-z</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt></ruby>？』<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>欠</rb><rt>*Kyʽin*</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一百簍</rb><rt>ih-pah-leo</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>把賬</rb><rt>*Pô-tsiang*</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>*sin-sang*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>票子</rb><rt>pʽiao-ts</rt><rb>馱仔</rb><rt>do-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>坐帶落</rb><rt>zo-tæn-lôh</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>五十簍</rb><rt>ng-jih-*leo*</rt></ruby>。』<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt></ruby>？』<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>麥</rb><rt>Mah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一百石</rb><rt>ih-pah-zah</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。』<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>票子</rb><rt>pʽiao-ts</rt><rb>馱仔</rb><rt>do-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>八十石</rb><rt>pah-jih-*zah*</rt></ruby>。』<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>良士</rb><rt>Lang-z</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>把賬</rb><rt>pô-tsiang</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>巧</rb><rt>kyʽiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>時世個</rb><rt>z-shü-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>關着</rb><rt>kwæn-djôh</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>箇代</rb><rt>keh-dæ</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>光明個</rb><rt>kwông-ming-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>巧</rb><rt>kyʽiao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Yiu-tʽa nying ih-zah, Ning-po ʽoh-ts s-zah-to. <u>猶太</u>人一石，寧波斛子四石多。（原註）</sup>

<sup>9</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>用場</rb><rt>*yüong-dziang*</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>馱來</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>結交</rb><rt>kyih-kyiao</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>永遠個</rb><rt>üong-yün-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>忠心</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>忠心</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>忠心</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>正個</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>*dzæ-veh*</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>？<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>別人個</rb><rt>bih-nying-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>忠心</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>爾自個</rb><rt>ng-zi-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>？<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>M-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>傭人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>惡愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>親近</rb><rt>tsʽing-gying</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看輕</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽing</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Peng-veng* *z-ngæn*, Ma-mo-na. 本文字眼，瑪摩那。（原註）</sup>

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>貪財個</rb><rt>tʽen-dzæ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>冷笑</rb><rt>lang-siao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>鄭重個</rb><rt>dzing-djong-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>看來</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>穢污個</rb><rt>w̆e-u-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>Lih-fah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>先知人個</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>傳落</rb><rt>djün-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>從此以後</rb><rt>dzong-tsʽ-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>神國個</rb><rt>Jing-koh-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>傳開去</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>個個</rb><rt>ko-ko</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>力</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>撐進去</rb><rt>tsʽang-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt><rb>一點</rb><rt>ih-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>天地</rb><rt>tʽin-di</rt><rb>廢掉</rb><rt>fi-diao</rt><rb>容易</rb><rt>yüong-yi</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>離掉</rb><rt>li-diao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>*lao-nyüing*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>離掉個</rb><rt>li-diao-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>*lao-nyüing*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> 「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>寶藍</rb><rt>pao-læn</rt><rb>緞</rb><rt>dön</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>細</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>麻布</rb><rt>mô-pu</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>喫勒</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-leh</rt><rb>奢華</rb><rt>shæ-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>窮人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>Lah-sah-lu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>渾身</rb><rt>*weng-sing*</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>瘡</rb><rt>tsʽông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>安勒</rb><rt>en-leh</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>有佬個</rb><rt>yiu-lao-go</rt><rb>桌凳</rb><rt>coh-teng</rt><rb>跌落個</rb><rt>tih-lôh-go</rt><rb>零碎</rb><rt>ling-se</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>馱來</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>黃狗</rb><rt>wun-kyi</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>舔舔</rb><rt>tʽin-tʽin</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>瘡</rb><rt>tsʽông</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>*ʽEo-deo*</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>窮人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>抱勒去</rb><rt>bao-leh-kyʽi</rt><rb>放勒</rb><rt>fông-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>胸脘頭</rb><rt>hyüong-kwun-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安葬好</rb><rt>en-tsông-hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>陰司裏</rb><rt>ing-s-li</rt><rb>喫苦</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kwʽu</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>遠遠</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>Lah-sah-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>胸脘頭</rb><rt>hyüong-kwun-deo</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>懷懷</rb><rt>wæ-wæ</rt><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>Tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>可憐</rb><rt>kʽo-lin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>Lah-sah-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>指頭尖</rb><rt>ts-deo-tsin</rt><rb>搵點</rb><rt>w̆eng-tin</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>舌頭</rb><rt>zih-deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>冷陰記</rb><rt>lang-ing-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>火焰</rb><rt>ho-yin</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>苦弗過</rb><rt>kwʽu-feh-ko</rt></ruby>。』<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>兒孫</rb><rt>Ng-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt><rb>活前</rb><rt>weh-zin</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>受過</rb><rt>ziu-ko</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>Lah-sah-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>難頭</rb><rt>næn-deo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>受過</rb><rt>*ziu-ko*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>安穩</rb><rt>en-w̆eng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>喫苦了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kwʽu-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>隔開</rb><rt>kah-kʽæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一埭</rb><rt>ih-da</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>坑</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>造成了</rb><rt>zao-dzing-liao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>蕩邊</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>箇邊</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇邊</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>蕩邊</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。』<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>Lah-sah-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>*eo*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>*Lah-sah-lu*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>勸勸</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>喫苦個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kwʽu-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。』<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>勸</rb><rt>*kyʽün*</rt></ruby>。』<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>Tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>覅</rb><rt>fæ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>。』<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>*Üô-pah-lah-hen*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>勸</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt></ruby>。』」

---

### Lk. XVII.

<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>帶累</rb><rt>Ta-li</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>𢲔跌</rb><rt>pæn-tih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>弄出來</rb><rt>long-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>帶累</rb><rt>Ta-li</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>𢲔跌</rb><rt>pæn-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>磨上層</rb><rt>mo-zông-zeng</rt><rb>掛</rb><rt>kwô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>頭頸</rb><rt>deo-kying</rt><rb>丟落</rb><rt>tiu-lôh</rt><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt><rb>刑罰</rb><rt>*ying-vah*</rt><rb>輕可</rb><rt>kyʽing-kʽo</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赦回</rb><rt>sô-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>回心轉意了</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>饒</rb><rt>nyiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>一日</rb><rt>Ih-nyih</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七遭</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsao</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>爾儂</rb><rt>ng-noh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>七遭</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsao</rt><rb>走轉來</rb><rt>tseo-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>回心轉意了</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i-de</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>卻勒</rb><rt>kyʽiah-leh</rt><rb>饒</rb><rt>nyiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>使徒</rb><rt>S-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>*Gyiu*</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>*Ng*</rt><rb>加湊</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>一粒</rb><rt>ih-lih</rt><rb>芥菜子</rb><rt>ka-tsʽæ-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇株</rb><rt>keh-cü</rt><rb>楓樹</rb><rt>fong-jü</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>根頭</rb><rt>Keng-deo</rt><rb>拔起</rb><rt>bah-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>種勒</rb><rt>cong-leh</rt><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>耕</rb><rt>kang</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>田頭</rb><rt>din-deo</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>馬上</rb><rt>mô-zông</rt><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>？』<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>下飯</rb><rt>ʽô-væn</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>*sin*</rt><rb>備好</rb><rt>be-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>喝個</rb><rt>hah-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>繳身帶</rb><rt>kyiao-sing-ta</rt><rb>繫仔</rb><rt>kyi-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>侍候東</rb><rt>z-ʽeo-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>爾自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>？』<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>過謝</rb><rt>ko-zia</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>𣍐個</rb><rt>ve-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>是介個</rb><rt>Z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>吩咐個</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做好</rb><rt>tso-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>*zi*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸用個</rb><rt>m-yüong-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做過個</rb><rt>tso-ko-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>門份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒瑪利亞</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>交界</rb><rt>kao-ka</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>Tseo-tsing</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>鄉村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>碰見</rb><rt>pʽong-kyin</rt><rb>十個</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>大麻風</rb><rt>da-mô-fong</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>遠遠</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>立間</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可憐</rb><rt>kʽo-lin</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>正好</rb><rt>tsing-hao</rt><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>乾淨了</rb><rt>ken-zing-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>內中</rb><rt>Nen-cong</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>好了</rb><rt>hao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>倒走轉</rb><rt>tao-tseo-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>腳下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謝謝</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒瑪利亞</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>和十個</rb><rt>ʽo-jih-go</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>乾淨了</rb><rt>ken-zing-de</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>九個頭</rb><rt>kyiu-go-den</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>Tsih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>外國人</rb><rt>nga-koh-nying</rt><rb>轉來</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>FAH-LI-SÆ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>幾時</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看勒見個</rb><rt>kʽen-leh-kyin-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt></ruby>！』<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>！』<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。」

<sup>22</sup> ---

### Lk. XVIII.

<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>懈怠</rb><rt>yiæ-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>理刑廳</rb><rt>li-ying-tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>愀睬</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>*Gyiu*</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>*ng*</rt><rb>申</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>冤</rb><rt>ün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>冤家個</rb><rt>ün-kô-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。』<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>官</rb><rt>*Kwun*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>愀睬</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>只管</rb><rt>*coh-kwun*</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>囉唆</rb><rt>l̆o-so</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>申</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>長通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>吵鬧</rb><rt>tsʽao-nao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>理刑廳</rb><rt>li-ying-tʽing</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>揀擇個</rb><rt>kæn-dzeh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>夜夜</rb><rt>yia-yia</rt><rb>懷懷</rb><rt>wæ-wæ</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-kôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>申</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>冤</rb><rt>ün</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>申</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>*kʽen-kyin*</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>弗起</rb><rt>feh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>：<sup>10</sup> 「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>收錢糧</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>謝謝</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>搶奪</rb><rt>tsʽiang-deh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>公道</rb><rt>kong-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>收錢糧</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>Me</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>禮拜</rb><rt>li-pa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩遭</rb><rt>liang-tsao</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十股</rb><rt>jih-kwu</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>一股</rb><rt>ih-kwu</rt></ruby>。』<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>收錢糧</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>遠遠</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djôh</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>胸脘頭</rb><rt>hyüong-kwun-deo</rt><rb>敲敲</rb><rt>kʽao-kʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>哦</rb><rt>ô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>可憐</rb><rt>kʽo-lin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>犯罪個</rb><rt>væn-ze-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。』<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>收錢糧</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>歸去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>降</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>低</rb><rt>ti</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>低</rb><rt>ti</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>升</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>*YIU-SING*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>抱勒</rb><rt>bao-leh</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>狠</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>攏來</rb><rt>long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇種介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神國</rb><rt>Jing-koh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>走進去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>YIU</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>問問</rb><rt>meng-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>？」<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>Ih-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>』『<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>』『<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>偷盜</rb><rt>tʽeo-dao</rt></ruby>』『<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>謊告</rb><rt>hwông-kao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>』『<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>父母</rb><rt>vu-meo</rt></ruby>』 」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>顧着個</rb><rt>kwu-djôh-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>統</rb><rt>tʽong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>窮人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>撒該</rb><rt>Sah-kæ</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>走落來</rb><rt>tseo-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>今末仔</rb><rt>kyih-mih-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒該</rb><rt>*Sah-kæ*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>落來</rb><rt>lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歡歡喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>宿夜</rb><rt>soh-yia</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒該</rb><rt>Sah-kæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>家計</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>窮人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>謊告</rb><rt>hwông-kao</rt><rb>索詐</rb><rt>soh-tsô</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>間</rb><rt>kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>Kyih-mih</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>一家</rb><rt>ih-kô</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>救星</rb><rt>kyiu-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>特爲</rb><rt>*deh-we*</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>失掉間個</rb><rt>shih-diao-kæn-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>顯出來</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>聽東</rb><rt>tʽing-tong</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，

<sup>12</sup> 「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>貴重個</rb><rt>kwe-djong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>遠遠</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>國王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt><rb>職份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>謳攏</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>十個</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>十塊</rb><rt>jih-kwʽe</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生意</rb><rt>sang-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>。』<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>惡愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>*tao*</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>*keh-deo*</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>*di-fông*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>。』<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>貴重個</rb><rt>kwe-djong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>受勒</rb><rt>ziu-leh</rt><rb>國王個</rb><rt>koh-wông-go</rt><rb>職份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>謳攏</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>賺頭</rb><rt>dzæn-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>頭一個</rb><rt>Deo-ih-go</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十塊</rb><rt>jih-kwʽe</rt><rb>賺頭</rb><rt>dzæn-deo</rt></ruby>。』<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>*Cü-nying-kô*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>好好</rb><rt>Hao-hao</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>小小</rb><rt>siao-siao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>忠心</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>掌管</rb><rt>công-kwun</rt><rb>十個</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>縣份</rb><rt>yün-veng</rt></ruby>。』<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>第二個</rb><rt>Di-nyi-go</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五塊</rb><rt>ng-kwʽe</rt><rb>賺頭</rb><rt>dzæn-deo</rt></ruby>。』<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>縣份</rb><rt>yün-veng</rt></ruby>。』<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>keh-kʽwe</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>包勒</rb><rt>pao-leh</rt><rb>手巾裏</rb><rt>siu-kying-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>厲害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>安間個</rb><rt>en-kæn-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>馱歸</rb><rt>do-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>種間個</rb><rt>cong-kæn-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>收割</rb><rt>siu-keh</rt></ruby>。』<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>*Cü-nying-kô*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>惡</rb><rt>ôh</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>爾自個</rb><rt>ng-zi-go</rt><rb>口供</rb><rt>kʽeo-kong</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>*Kyi-jün*</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>厲害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>安間個</rb><rt>en-kæn-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>馱歸</rb><rt>do-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>種間個</rb><rt>cong-kæn-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>收割</rb><rt>siu-keh</rt></ruby>，<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>存勒</rb><rt>dzeng-leh</rt><rb>錢店裏</rb><rt>dzin-tin-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>利鈿</rb><rt>li-din</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>收歸</rb><rt>siu-kyü</rt></ruby>？』<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>*Cü-nying-kô*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>立東個</rb><rt>lih-tong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十塊頭</rb><rt>jih-kwʽe-den</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。』<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十塊</rb><rt>jih-kwʽe</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>！』<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>*Cü-nying-kô*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>加湊</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>奪勒去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>國王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>揸勒</rb><rt>cô-leh</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>\[1\] Me-kwʽe da-iah jih-to-liang djong. 每塊大約十多兩重。（原註）</sup>

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>講好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>KWʽA</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯法基</rb><rt>Pah-fah-kyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯大尼</rb><rt>Pah-da-nyi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>（<ruby><rb>箇座</rb><rt>*keh-zo*</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>橄欖</rb><rt>Ken-læn</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>*sæn*</rt></ruby>），<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>30</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>對頭個</rb><rt>te-deo-go</rt><rb>鄉村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>一匹</rb><rt>ih-pʽih</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>樁間</rb><rt>công-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從來</rb><rt>dzong-læ</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>騎過</rb><rt>gyi-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>解仔</rb><rt>ka-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牽勒來</rb><rt>kyʽin-leh-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>Tso</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>*li-ts*</rt></ruby>？』<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用着</rb><rt>yüong-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>差去個</rb><rt>Tsʽa-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>*keh-deo*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>果然</rb><rt>*ko-jün*</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>居</rb><rt>ke</rt><rb>箇匹</rb><rt>keh-pʽih</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用着</rb><rt>yüong-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>牽勒</rb><rt>kyʽin-leh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>擋勒</rb><rt>tông-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>騎上去</rb><rt>gyi-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>鋪</rb><rt>pʽu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>路裏</rb><rt>lu-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>近攏</rb><rt>gying-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>*Yiæ-lu-sah-leng*</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>橄欖</rb><rt>Ken-læn</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>看見過個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>有夥</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>歡歡喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>38</sup> 「<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>Kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>祝讚個</rb><rt>coh-tsæn-go</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>但願</rb><rt>*dæn-nyün*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>太平</rb><rt>tʽa-bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>但願</rb><rt>*dæn-nyün*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>！」<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一聲</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>40</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>謳出來</rb><rt>eo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>Yi-kying</rt><rb>近了</rb><rt>gying-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>42</sup> 「<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>Kyih-mih</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>關着</rb><rt>kwæn-djôh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>太平</rb><rt>tʽa-bing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>*ziu*</rt><rb>好了</rb><rt>*hao-de*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>遮住了</rb><rt>tsô-djü-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>扒</rb><rt>bô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>平</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾儂</rb><rt>ng-noh</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>庉的個</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連即</rb><rt>*lin-tsih*</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-lôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>上頂</rb><rt>zông-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>看顧</rb><rt>kʽen-kwu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>45</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>買賣</rb><rt>ma-ma</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>禱告個</rb><rt>tao-kao-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>。』 <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>賊窠</rb><rt>zeh-kʽo</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>47</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>Keh-pæn</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>出頭出腦</rb><rt>cʽih-deo-cʽih-nao</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>打算弗出</rb><rt>tang-sön-feh-cʽih</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>弄法</rb><rt>long-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>惡</rb><rt>ôh</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

### Lk. XX.
