<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb><u>約翰</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>*læ-go*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？』<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>*læ-go*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞煞</rb><rt>kʽang-sah</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>種</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>一園</rb><rt>ih-yün</rt><rb>紫葡萄</rb><rt>ts-bu-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>租出</rb><rt>tsu-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>種田人</rb><rt>cong-din-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>出門</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>庉了</rb><rt>deng-leh</rt><rb>長久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>*siu*</rt><rb>租</rb><rt>*tsu*</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>種田人</rb><rt>cong-din-nying</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>給</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>園裏個</rb><rt>yün-li-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>種田人</rb><rt>cong-din-nying</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>空手</rb><rt>kʽong-siu</rt><rb>歸去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>霉</rb><rt>me</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>空手</rb><rt>kʽong-siu</rt><rb>歸去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>第三遭</rb><rt>Di-sæn-tsao</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>竟是介</rb><rt>kying-z-ka</rt><rb>打打傷</rb><rt>tang-tang-sông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>居園個</rb><rt>Ke-yün-go</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>*keh-tsao*</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>值鈿個</rb><rt>dzih-din-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要防</rb><rt>iao-bông</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。』<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>種田人</rb><rt>Cong-din-nying</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>殺勒</rb><rt>sah-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。』<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>推</rb><rt>tʽe</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>園外</rb><rt>yün-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>居園個</rb><rt>ke-yün-go</rt><rb>業主</rb><rt>nyih-cü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>？<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>種田人</rb><rt>cong-din-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>葡萄園</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽聞仔</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb>起屋個</rb><rt>Kyʽi-oh-go</rt><rb>師父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>*oh*</rt><rb>角落頭</rb><rt>kôh-lôh-den</rt><rb>頭一塊</rb><rt>deo-ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>』 <ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>石頭上</rb><rt>zah-deo-zông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>跌糊</rb><rt>tih-wu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>*keh-kwʽe*</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>*zah-deo*</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>壓勒</rb><rt>ah-leh</rt><rb>粉碎</rb><rt>feng-se</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>當時</rb><rt>tông-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>下手</rb><rt>ʽô-siu</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>趁此機會</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽ-kyi-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>探聽</rb><rt>tʽen-tʽing</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>假充</rb><rt>kô-cʽong</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>訛頭</rb><rt>ngo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>送去</rb><rt>song-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>總督個</rb><rt>tsong-toh-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只勒</rb><rt>tsih-leh</rt><rb>實實惠惠</rb><rt>jih-jih-we-we</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>投</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>錢糧</rb><rt>zin-liang</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>奸計</rb><rt>kæn-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>試試</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>？<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>Do</rt><rb>十塊</rb><rt>jih-kwʽe</rt><rb>番餅</rb><rt>fæn-ping</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>頭子</rb><rt>Deo-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>國號</rb><rt>koh-ʽao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰儂個</rb><rt>jü-noh-go</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>該撒</u>個</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>該撒</u>個</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>*peh*</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>訛頭</rb><rt>ngo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗響了</rb><rt>feh-hyiang-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答個</rb><rt>we-teh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Keh-kwʽe* *fæn-ping* *dzih* *ih-din* *nying-ts*. 該塊番餅值一鈿銀子。（原註）</sup>

<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒土該</rb><rt>Sah-tʽu-kæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>活轉來個</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>28</sup> 「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>寫落</rb><rt>sia-lôh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>剩勒</rb><rt>dzing-leh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt><rb>傳宗接代</rb><rt>djün-cong-tsih-dæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一份</rb><rt>*ih-veng*</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>抬了</rb><rt>dæ-leh</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>抬了</rb><rt>dæ-leh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>第三</rb><rt>Di-sæn</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>和七個</rb><rt>ʽo-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>交落</rb><rt>gao-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>頂</rb><rt>Ting</rt><rb>結煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>活轉來個</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>誰個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>時世個</rb><rt>z-shü-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>娶進</rb><rt>cʽü-tsing</rt><rb>嫁出</rb><rt>kô-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>時世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>娶進</rb><rt>cʽü-tsing</rt><rb>嫁出</rb><rt>kô-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活轉來個</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>一樣個</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb>刺棚</rb><rt>Tsʽ-bang</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>』<ruby><rb>箇篇</rb><rt>keh-pʽin</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>指點過</rb><rt>ts-tin-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>以撒</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。』<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>死人個</rb><rt>si-nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活人個</rb><rt>weh-nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>看來</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, gyi-lah long-tsong we-leh Jing-ming weh-go: *kʽen* Lm. 6. 10, 11. 或話，其拉攏總爲了神明活個。看 Lm. 6. 10, 11.（原註）</sup>

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>Yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>Nying-kô</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>？<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖詩裏</rb><rt>Sing-s-li</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>踏腳凳</rb><rt>dah-kyiah-teng</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>*kyi-jün*</rt><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>聽東</rb><rt>tʽing-tong</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>46</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>謹防</rb><rt>kying-bông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>長長</rb><rt>dziang-dziang</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>走來走去</rb><rt>tseo-læ-tseo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>市面上</rb><rt>z-min-zông</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>上位</rb><rt>zông-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>酒筵上</rb><rt>tsiu-yin-zông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>頭位</rb><rt>deo-we</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>吞喫</rb><rt>tʽeng-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𡢿個</rb><rt>lao-nyüing-go</rt><rb>家計</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>假戲頭</rb><rt>kô-hyi-deo</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>漫長</rb><rt>mæn-dziang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>受罪</rb><rt>ziu-ze</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>。」

### Lk. XXI.

<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>丟落</rb><rt>tiu-lôh</rt><rb>錢庫裏</rb><rt>dzin-kwʽu-li</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>窮</rb><rt>gyüong</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-ten</rt><rb>丟</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>銅鈿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>實惠</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>窮窮</rb><rt>gyüong-gyüong</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>丟的個</rb><rt>tiu-tih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>多頭</rb><rt>to-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>丟點的</rb><rt>tiu-tin-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>缺利利</rb><rt>kyʽüih-li-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>丟進的了</rb><rt>tiu-tsing-tih-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Keh* *liang* *dong-din* *dzih* *ih-li* *nying-ts*: *kʽen* Mk. 12. 42. 箇兩銅鈿值一釐銀子。看 Mk. 12. 42.（原註）</sup>

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>YIU</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>助攏來個</rb><rt>dzu-long-læ-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>裝飾東</rb><rt>tsông-sih-tong</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>6</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>看個</rb><rt>kʽen-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>到其</rb><rt>tao-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連只</rb><rt>*lin-tsih*</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-lôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>上頂</rb><rt>zông-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拆掉</rb><rt>tsʽah-diao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>幾時</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>兆頭</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>帶掉</rb><rt>ta-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>冒勒</rb><rt>mao-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>來其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>近了</rb><rt>gying-de</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。』<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>繚亂個</rb><rt>liao-lön-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>末個</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>馬上</rb><rt>mô-zông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>*ziu*</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>*tao*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, yi *wô*, Z-ʽeo gying-de. Kʽen Mt. 3. 2: 4. 17. 或話，又話，時候近了。看 Mt. 3. 2: 4. 17.（原註）</sup>

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>*feh-zing*</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>下手</rb><rt>ʽô-siu</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>趕勒害</rb><rt>ken-leh-ʽæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>聚會堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>挈</rb><rt>kyʽih</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>牢監</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>總督個</rb><rt>tsong-toh-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>*pʽong-djôh*</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>*keh-sing*</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>機會</rb><rt>kyi-we</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>*peh*</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>抲定</rb><rt>kʽô-ding</rt><rb>甮</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>分剖</rb><rt>feng-pʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>口才</rb><rt>kʽeo-dzæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>對頭</rb><rt>te-deo</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>駁</rb><rt>poh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗倒</rb><rt>feh-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>對敵</rb><rt>te-dih</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗過</rb><rt>feh-ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>親眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>*kwun*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>刻忖</rb><rt>kʽeh-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>*lin*</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>頭裏個</rb><rt>deo-li-go</rt><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>失掉</rb><rt>shih-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>保全</rb><rt>pao-djün</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>兵馬</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>圍住</rb><rt>we-djü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>化爲平地</rb><rt>hwô-we-bing-di</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>近了</rb><rt>gying-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>逃</rb><rt>dao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>山裏</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>城中</rb><rt>dzing-cong</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>鄉個</rb><rt>hyiang-go</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>走進去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>申冤</rb><rt>sing-ün</rt><rb>還報個</rb><rt>wæn-pao-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效驗</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大肚</rb><rt>do-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>喫奶個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-na-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>遭</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>大劫</rb><rt>do-kyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>刀口裏</rb><rt>Tao-kʽeo-li</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>擄掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>萬國</rb><rt>væn-koh</rt><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>踏掉</rb><rt>dah-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>別國人個</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>滿出</rb><rt>mun-cʽih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> 「<ruby><rb>日頭</rb><rt>Nyih-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>月亮</rb><rt>yüih-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>星宿</rb><rt>sing-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>兆頭</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>遭劫</rb><rt>tsao-kyih</rt><rb>嘸設法</rb><rt>m-shih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>海洋</rb><rt>hæ-yiang</rt><rb>風浪</rb><rt>fong-lông</rt><rb>牙牙響</rb><rt>ngô-ngô-hyiang</rt></ruby>；<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>*keh-sing*</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>*gyi-z*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>防到</rb><rt>bông-tao</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嚇勒</rb><rt>hah-leh</rt><rb>失魂憐氣</rb><rt>shih-weng-lin-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>搖動</rb><rt>yiao-dong</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>發作</rb><rt>fah-tsôh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>*kyi-sing*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>直起來</rb><rt>dzih-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>抬起來</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>取贖</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>無花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百樣</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>樹木</rb><rt>jü-moh</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>爆出</rb><rt>pao-cʽih</rt><rb>芽頭</rb><rt>*ngô-den*</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>夏天</rb><rt>ʽô-tʽin</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>是介個</rb><rt>Z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>動作起來</rb><rt>dong-tsôh-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>過去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>Tʽin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>過去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>過去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尚恐怕</rb><rt>zông-kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>今世個</rb><rt>kying-si-go</rt><rb>愁悶</rb><rt>zeo-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>呆頓頓</rb><rt>ngæ-teng-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>突然</rb><rt>deh-jün</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>弶</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>鳥獸</rb><rt>tiao-siu</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>*nyih-ts*</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>*ih*</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>*tao*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>普天地下</rb><rt>pʽu-tʽin-di-ʽô</rt><rb>安耽</rb><rt>en-tæn</rt><rb>庉的個</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>罩進</rb><rt>tsao-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>甦醒</rb><rt>su-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>避掉</rb><rt>bi-diao</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每夜</rb><rt>me-yia</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>橄欖</rb><rt>Ken-læn</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>*sæn*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>宿夜</rb><rt>soh-yia</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。

### Lk. XXII.

<ruby><rb>無酵餅個</rb><rt>VU-KAO-PING-GO</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>逾越節</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>十二個</rb><rt>Jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>加略</u>個</rb><rt>Kyüô-liah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>入進</rb><rt>jih-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>侍衛</rb><rt>z-we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>講開</rb><rt>kông-kʽæ</rt><rb>給</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>*Yiu-da*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>應承</rb><rt>ing-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>鬧事</rb><rt>nao-z</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>機會</rb><rt>kyi-we</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, cong-nying m-neh læ-tong go kyi-we. 或話，衆人嘸得來東個機會。（原註）</sup>

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>無酵節</rb><rt>VU-KAO-TSIH</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一瓶</rb><rt>ih-bing</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>挈的</rb><rt>kyʽih-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走進個</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>進去</rb><rt>tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇份</rb><rt>keh-veng</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt><rb>箇間</rb><rt>keh-kæn</rt><rb>客房</rb><rt>kʽah-vông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？』<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>擺設好了</rb><rt>pa-shih-hao-liao</rt><rb>一間</rb><rt>ih-kæn</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>樓頂</rb><rt>leo-teng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>備好間</rb><rt>be-hao-kæn</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>果然</rb><rt>*ko-jün*</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt><rb>備好間</rb><rt>be-hao-kæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>Z-ʽeo</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>坐席</rb><rt>zo-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>坐席</rb><rt>*zo-zih*</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>企慕</rb><rt>kyʽi-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>難</rb><rt>næn</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從今以後</rb><rt>dzong-kying-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神國</rb><rt>Jing-koh</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效驗</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>接受了</rb><rt>tsih-ziu-leh</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝謝好</rb><rt>coh-zia-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>分帶開</rb><rt>feng-tæn-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>葡萄</rb><rt>bu-dao</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>饅頭</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝謝仔</rb><rt>coh-zia-ts</rt><rb>𤖼開</rb><rt>pʽah-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>捨出個</rb><rt>sô-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>逾越</rb><rt>*Yü-yüih*</rt><rb>喫好仔</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>*do-leh*</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>簇嶄新個</rb><rt>tsʽoh-tsæn-sing-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>設立個</rb><rt>*shih-lih-go*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>流出來個</rb><rt>liu-cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>桌凳上</rb><rt>coh-teng-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>原是</rb><rt>nyün-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>定好了個</rb><rt>ding-hao-liao-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！」<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>問來問去</rb><rt>meng-læ-meng-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>爭論</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>別國個</rb><rt>bih-koh-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>壓實</rb><rt>ah-jih</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>執掌</rb><rt>tsih-công</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>管住</rb><rt>kwun-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>恩人</rb><rt>eng-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>是介個</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>*kæ*</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>當值個</rb><rt>tông-dzih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>坐席個</rb><rt>zo-zih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>當值個</rb><rt>tông-dzih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>坐席個</rb><rt>zo-zih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>當值個</rb><rt>tông-dzih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>遭難個</rb><rt>tsao-næn-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>長通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>約好</rb><rt>iah-hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>，<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>國裏</rb><rt>koh-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做桌</rb><rt>tso-coh</rt><rb>喫喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>喝喝</rb><rt>hah-hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>座位裏</rb><rt>zo-we-li</rt><rb>審判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>*gyün-ping*</rt><rb>討過了</rb><rt>tʽao-ko-de</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>簸攏</rb><rt>po-long</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>麥</rb><rt>mah</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾儂</rb><rt>ng-noh</rt><rb>求過了</rb><rt>gyiu-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>失掉</rb><rt>shih-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>復轉來</rb><rt>voh-cün-læ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>堅固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>兄弟個</rb><rt>hyüong-di-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>*sing*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>落監</rb><rt>lôh-kæn</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>情願個</rb><rt>dzing-nyün-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今末仔</rb><rt>Kyih-mih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雞</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>啼個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>賞滿</rb><rt>sông-mun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>包袱</rb><rt>pao-voh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>鞋</rb><rt>ʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>缺少</rb><rt>kyʽüih-siao</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>M-kao</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>*kyʽüih*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>有勒</rb><rt>yiu-leh</rt><rb>賞滿</rb><rt>sông-mun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>帶勒去</rb><rt>ta-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有勒</rb><rt>*yiu-leh*</rt><rb>包袱</rb><rt>pao-voh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介個</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>*tao*</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>一把</rb><rt>ih-pô</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>算進</rb><rt>sön-tsing</rt><rb>在內</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>』 <ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>應着</rb><rt>ing-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>關着</rb><rt>kwæn-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>完全</rb><rt>wun-djün</rt></ruby>。」<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩把</rb><rt>liang-pô</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>夠了</rb><rt>Keo-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出仔</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>閒常</rb><rt>ʽæn-djông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>橄欖</rb><rt>Ken-læn</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>迷惑</rb><rt>mi-ʽôh</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>約來</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞起</rb><rt>kʽang-kyʽi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt><rb>遠</rb><rt>*yün*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>腳䯊頭</rb><rt>kyiah-kʽo-deo</rt><rb>跪落</rb><rt>gyü-lôh</rt><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>42</sup> 「<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>免掉</rb><rt>min-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>*gyiu*</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>*Ng*</rt><rb>免掉仔</rb><rt>*min-diao-ts*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>*cü-i*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>現出來</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>力道</rb><rt>lih-dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>難過</rb><rt>næn-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>極頭</rb><rt>gyih-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分外</rb><rt>veng-nga</rt><rb>求勒</rb><rt>gyiu-leh</rt><rb>急</rb><rt>kyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>汗</rb><rt>ʽen</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>點打點</rb><rt>tin-tang-tin</rt><rb>凝攏了</rb><rt>nying-long-liao</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>渧落</rb><rt>ti-lôh</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>求好了</rb><rt>Gyiu-hao-de</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>擔</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt><rb>睏熟的</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-tih</rt></ruby>，<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>睏熟東</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-tong</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>迷惑</rb><rt>mi-ʽôh</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, Ng ziah kʽeng, keh-go pe-ts teng Ngô do-ko. 或話，爾若肯，箇個杯子等我馱過。（原註）</sup>

<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一潮</rb><rt>ih-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>*tao-de*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>領前</rb><rt>ling-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>嗅嘴</rb><rt>hyüong-cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>嗅嘴</rb><rt>hyüong-cü</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>49</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>身邊</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做出來</rb><rt>tso-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>動</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>斬</rb><rt>tsæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>其中</rb><rt>Gyi-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>斬</rb><rt>tsæn</rt><rb>祭司頭個</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>削落</rb><rt>siah-lôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>順隻</rb><rt>jing-tsah</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt></ruby>。<sup>51</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>候</rb><rt>ʽEo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>52</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聖殿裏個</rb><rt>sing-din-li-go</rt><rb>侍衛</rb><rt>z-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>棍子</rb><rt>kweng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>強盜</rb><rt>gyiang-dao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>？<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>下手</rb><rt>ʽô-siu</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇晌</rb><rt>keh-zông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt><rb>地方個</rb><rt>*di-fông-go*</rt><rb>勢道</rb><rt>shü-dao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>54</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>帶勒去</rb><rt>ta-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>祭司頭個</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>衙門裏</rb><rt>ngô-meng-li</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>遠遠</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>跟間</rb><rt>keng-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>55</sup> <ruby><rb>明堂</rb><rt>Ming-dông</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>生勒</rb><rt>sang-leh</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>坐間</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djôh</rt><rb>火光</rb><rt>ho-kwông</rt><rb>坐間</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來間個</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>57</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>58</sup> <ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做隊</rb><rt>tso-de</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby>。」<sup>59</sup> <ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>時辰</rb><rt>z-zing</rt><rb>介相貌</rb><rt>ka-siang-mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>着實</rb><rt>dziah-jih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>同隊個</rb><rt>dong-de-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>60</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>懂</rb><rt>tong</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雞</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>啼了</rb><rt>di-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>61</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>扭轉</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>記得到</rb><rt>kyi-teh-tao</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>雞</rb><rt>Kyi</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>啼個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>62</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>哭勒</rb><rt>kʽoh-leh</rt><rb>傷心</rb><rt>sông-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>63</sup> <ruby><rb>管守</rb><rt>Kwun-siu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>戲弄</rb><rt>hyi-long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>64</sup> <ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>遮仔</rb><rt>tsô-ts</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>巴掌</rb><rt>pô-công</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>講講</rb><rt>kông-kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<sup>65</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>別樣告</rb><rt>bih-nyiang-kao</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>褻瀆</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>66</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>TAO-LEH</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>*cong*</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聚攏來</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>公會裏</rb><rt>kong-we-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>*meng*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>67</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>68</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>69</sup> <ruby><rb>從今以後</rb><rt>Dzong-kying-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>。」<sup>70</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>話着了</rb><rt>wô-djôh-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>71</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>何用</rb><rt>ʽo-yüong</rt><rb>別樣</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>親口</rb><rt>tsʽing-kʽeo</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>聽聞了</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-de</rt></ruby>。」
