### Lk. XXIII.

<ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>Keh-pæn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>靠實</rb><rt>kʽao-jih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>搖惑</rb><rt>yiao-ʽôh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>禁住</rb><rt>kying-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>甮</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>投</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>錢糧</rb><rt>zin-liang</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>稱</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>話着了</rb><rt>wô-djôh-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>拼命</rb><rt>pʽing-ming</rt><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>挑動</rb><rt>tʽiao-dong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咸到處</rb><rt>ʽen-tao-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>開檯</rb><rt>kʽæ-dæ</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>三個</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>字</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>該管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>長遠了</rb><rt>dziang-yün-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>*Hyi-lih*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>應</rb><rt>ing</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拼命</rb><rt>pʽing-ming</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>嘲笑</rb><rt>tsao-siao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>戲弄</rb><rt>hyi-long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一件</rb><rt>ih-gyin</rt><rb>雍雍</rb><rt>üong-üong</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>披勒</rb><rt>pʽi-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>*dzing-gyiu*</rt><rb>送去</rb><rt>song-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>怨氣</rb><rt>ün-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>和攏了</rb><rt>ʽo-long-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>朝攏</rb><rt>dziao-long</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>搖惑</rb><rt>yiao-ʽôh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>當面</rb><rt>tông-min</rt><rb>審過了</rb><rt>sing-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介個</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>*dziu*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>*Hyi-lih*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>判斷</rb><rt>*pʽun-tön*</rt><rb>看來</rb><rt>*kʽen-læ*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>打過仔</rb><rt>tang-ko-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> （<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>節肯個</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>務必子</rb><rt>vu-pih-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。）<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>合口齊動</rb><rt>ʽeh-kʽeo-zi-dong</rt><rb>懷起來</rb><rt>wæ-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>Kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉巴</rb><rt>Pô-lah-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*Keh-go*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉巴</rb><rt>*Pô-lah-pô*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>造反</rb><rt>dzao-fæn</rt><rb>殺人個</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt><rb>挈落</rb><rt>kyʽih-lôh</rt><rb>牢監</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一番</rb><rt>ih-fæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>懷起</rb><rt>wæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>釘</rb><rt>Ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>！」<sup>22</sup> <sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>帶勒去</rb><rt>ta-leh-kyʽi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>古利奈</rb><rt>Kwu-li-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>*kyiao-leh*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>田畈裏</rb><rt>din-pæn-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>抲勒</rb><rt>kʽô-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>放勒</rb><rt>fông-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>揹勒</rb><rt>pe-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>後背</rb><rt>ʽeo-pe</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>還有星</rb><rt>wa-yiu-sing</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>跟間</rb><rt>keng-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>敲敲</rb><rt>kʽao-kʽao</rt><rb>胸脘頭</rb><rt>*hyüong-kwun-deo*</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djôh</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>！』<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>蓋住</rb><rt>kæ-djü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>！』<sup>31</sup> <sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>拖勒去</rb><rt>tʽo-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>份齊</rb><rt>veng-zi</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>TSEO</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>骷髏</rb><rt>Kwʽu-leo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>*liang*</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>借手邊</rb><rt>tsia-siu-pin</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>饒赦</rb><rt>nyiao-sô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>*gyi-zi*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>撮</rb><rt>tsʽeh</rt><rb>指末頭</rb><rt>ts-meh-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>取笑</rb><rt>cʽü-siao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走攏去</rb><rt>tseo-long-kyʽi</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>酸酒</rb><rt>sön-tsiu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>*kyʽüoh*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>37</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一張</rb><rt>ih-tsiang</rt><rb>條子</rb><rt>diao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希利尼</rb><rt>Hyi-li-nyi</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb><u>希伯來</u>個</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ-go</rt><rb>字眼</rb><rt>z-ngæn</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>KEH</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>YIU-TʽA</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>NYING-GO</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>WÔNG-TI</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>*liang*</rt><rb>掛間個</rb><rt>kwô-kæn-go</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>褻瀆</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>第二個</rb><rt>Di-nyi-go</rt><rb>應起來</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>責罰</rb><rt>tsah-vah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>定了</rb><rt>ding-leh</rt><rb>一樣個</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>！<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>應該個</rb><rt>ing-kæ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>受個</rb><rt>ziu-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>報應</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>做賺</rb><rt>tso-dzæn</rt></ruby>。」<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>執掌</rb><rt>tsih-công</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>國家個</rb><rt>koh-kô-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>記</rb><rt>kyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>43</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今末仔</rb><rt>Kyih-mih-ts</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>福園</rb><rt>foh-yün</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>Jih-nyi</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>光景</rb><rt>kwông-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>遍地</rb><rt>pin-di</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>暗</rb><rt>*en*</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>日頭</rb><rt>Nyih-deo</rt><rb>變勒</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>暗</rb><rt>en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聖殿裏個</rb><rt>*sing*-din-li-go</rt><rb>幔帳</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>𠡒裂開</rb><rt>te-lih-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>活靈</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。』 」<ruby><rb>講好</rb><rt>Kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>氣</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>斷了</rb><rt>dön-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Yiu-teh fæn, pin-tʽin-di-ʽô. Mt. Mk. yia z-ka. 有得翻，遍天地下。Mt. Mk. 也是介。（原註）</sup>

<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>百總</rb><rt>Pah-tsong</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>Tseo-long-læ</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>敲敲</rb><rt>kʽao-kʽao</rt><rb>胸脘頭</rb><rt>hyüong-kwun-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>歸去了</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>49</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相熟個</rb><rt>siang-joh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>跟勒來個</rb><rt>keng-leh-læ-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>遠遠</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>立東</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。

<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>YIU</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>議事部裏個</rb><rt>nyi-z-bu-li-go</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<sup>51</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>徇情</rb><rt>shing-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞利馬太</rb><rt>Üô-li-mô-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城裏個</rb><rt>zing-li-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>神國</rb><rt>Jing-koh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走進去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>討</rb><rt>tʽao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt></ruby>。<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>馱落來</rb><rt>do-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>細</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>麻布</rb><rt>mô-pu</rt><rb>裹好仔</rb><rt>ko-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安勒</rb><rt>en-leh</rt><rb>石巖</rb><rt>zah-ngæn</rt><rb>鑿出來個</rb><rt>zôh-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>墳</rb><rt>veng</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>葬過</rb><rt>tsông-ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>54</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>預備日</rb><rt>yü-be-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>近了</rb><rt>gying-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>55</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>墳墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>葬法</rb><rt>tsông-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>歸去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>香料</rb><rt>hyiang-liao</rt><rb>沒藥</rb><rt>meh-yiah</rt><rb>備好仔</rb><rt>be-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>庉過</rb><rt>deng-ko</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>。

---

### Lk. XXIV.

<ruby><rb>七日個</rb><rt>Tsʽih-nyih-go</rt><rb>頭日</rb><rt>deo-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>黑早</rb><rt>heh-tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>備好間個</rb><rt>be-hao-kæn-go</rt><rb>香料</rb><rt>hyiang-liao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>墳頭</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>Kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>墳</rb><rt>veng</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>*meng*-kʽeo</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>翻過了</rb><rt>fæn-ko-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>走進去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>正好</rb><rt>tsing-hao</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>疑惑不決</rb><rt>nyi-ʽôh-peh-kyüih</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>雪刮亮</rb><rt>shih-kwah-liang</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>忽然</rb><rt>hweh-jün</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>怕起來</rb><rt>pʽô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>傴倒</rb><rt>eo-tao</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>*liang-go*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>死人</rb><rt>Si-nying</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>活人</rb><rt>weh-nying</rt></ruby>？<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活轉來了</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>講過了</rb><rt>kông-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>7</sup> 『<ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>罪人個</rb><rt>ze-nying-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>釘煞</rb><rt>ting-sah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>*Nyü-nying*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>記得到</rb><rt>kyi-teh-tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>墳頭</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>轉來</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>十一個</rb><rt>jih-ih-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>抹大拉</u>個</rb><rt>Meh-da-lah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約亞拿</rb><rt>Iah-üô-nô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>*ah-nyiang*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來間個</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>女人個</rb><rt>Nyü-nying-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>看來</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>惡像</rb><rt>ôh-ziang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>謠言</rb><rt>yiao-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立起身</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-sing</rt><rb>奔</rb><rt>peng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>墳墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>傴倒</rb><rt>eo-tao</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>麻布</rb><rt>mô-pu</rt><rb>安間</rb><rt>en-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>KEH</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>鄉村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>廿五</rb><rt>nyiæn-ng</rt><rb>里</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以馬忤</rb><rt>Yi-mô-ngwu</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>依路</rb><rt>*i-lu*</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講講</rb><rt>kông-kông</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>近來</rb><rt>gying-læ</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>正好</rb><rt>Tsing-hao</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>議論</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>近攏去</rb><rt>gying-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>迷住了</rb><rt>mi-djü-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>憂悶相</rb><rt>iu-meng-siang</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>講來講去</rb><rt>kông-læ-kông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>？」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革流巴</rb><rt>Keh-liu-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>耽擱間</rb><rt>tæn-kôh-kæn</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要防</rb><rt>*iao-bông*</rt><rb>只勒</rb><rt>tsih-leh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>城裏個</rb><rt>zing-li-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>！」<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>啥個</rb><rt>Soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>Kông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>*peh*</rt><rb>總督</rb><rt>*tsong-toh*</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>取贖</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>*pah-sing*</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>墳頭</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>*liang-go*</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>現出來</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>活了</rb><rt>weh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>隊夥</rb><rt>de-ho</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>墳頭</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>果然</rb><rt>*ko-jün*</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>一樣個</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。」

<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>愚笨個</rb><rt>Nyü-beng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt><rb>難得</rb><rt>næn-teh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>話過個</rb><rt>wô-ko-go</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>！<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>*sin*</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>苦難</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>享</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>應該個</rb><rt>ing-kæ-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>聖書裏</rb><rt>Sing-shü-li</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>詳細</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>Kwʽa</rt><rb>近攏</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>鄉村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>惡像</rb><rt>ôh-ziang</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>走過去</rb><rt>tseo-ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>煞扣</rb><rt>sah-kʽeo</rt><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>*Yin-dzæ*</rt><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>打笡</rb><rt>tang-tsʽia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>夜了</rb><rt>yia-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉東</rb><rt>deng-tong</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>走進去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>坐席個</rb><rt>zo-zih-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>饅頭</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝福仔</rb><rt>coh-foh-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𤖼開</rb><rt>pʽah-kʽæ</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>開了</rb><rt>kʽæ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>忽而然</rb><rt>hweh-r-jün</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗見</rb><rt>feh-kyin</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>路上</rb><rt>Lu-zông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>熱個</rb><rt>nyih-go</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>當時</rb><rt>tông-z</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>十一個</rb><rt>jih-ih-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>隊夥</rb><rt>de-ho</rt><rb>聚攏間</rb><rt>jü-long-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正好</rb><rt>tsing-hao</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<sup>34</sup> 「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活轉來了</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見過了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>路上個</rb><rt>lu-zông-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>𤖼開</rb><rt>pʽah-kʽæ</rt><rb>饅頭</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>話出來了</rb><rt>wô-cʽih-læ-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>正</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>平安</rb><rt>bing-en</rt></ruby>！」<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>喫一驚</rb><rt>kyʽih-ih-kying</rt><rb>怕起來了</rb><rt>pʽô-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>定道</rb><rt>ding-dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>陰魂</rb><rt>ing-weng</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>懍懍動</rb><rt>ling-ling-dong</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>疑惑個</rb><rt>nyi-ʽôh-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>？<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>Ngô-zi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>摸摸</rb><rt>moh-moh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>靠實</rb><rt>kʽao-jih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>陰魂</rb><rt>ing-weng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>骨頭</rb><rt>kweh-deo</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>講好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>馱點</rb><rt>do-tin</rt><rb>炙熟了個</rb><rt>tsih-joh-liao-go</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>蜜蜂窠</rb><rt>mih-fong-kʽo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>馱仔</rb><rt>do-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>喫了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>講過了</rb><rt>kông-ko-liao</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>先知人個</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>*shü*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>聖詩</rb><rt>Sing-s</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效驗</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt><rb>聖書個</rb><rt>Sing-shü-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>難</rb><rt>næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>*kæ*</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt><rb>赦罪個</rb><rt>sô-ze-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>萬國</rb><rt>væn-koh</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹個</rb><rt>z-ken-go</rt><rb>見證</rb><rt>kyin-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>應許個</rb><rt>ing-hyü-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜落</rb><rt>s-lôh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>50</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>領勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯大尼</rb><rt>Pah-da-nyi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>兩隻</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>提帶起</rb><rt>di-tæn-kyʽi</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>51</sup> <ruby><rb>正好</rb><rt>Tsing-hao</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歡歡喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>祝謝</rb><rt>coh-zia</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亞們</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。
